# Scorcher (Zyrinx / Scavenger, 1996-97) — reverse-engineering notes

Started 2026-10-09. Non-commercial. Working from the retail Saturn disc + (to find) the PC install.

## Saturn disc (MK-81214 V2.000, built 1997-02-20, regions JTUBKAEL)
- Copied to `saturn/cd/` (157 files, 17 MB). Boot header in `saturn/IP.BIN` (product-name field literally says "FUCK MAN").
- Layout: track 1 data (8538 sectors), track 2 one CD-audio track (42917 sectors, ~9.5 min). Ripped to `saturn/image/Scorcher.cue` (MODE1/2048 + raw audio, 0 read errors).
- Root binaries are SH-2 (GCC-style prologues `2f86 2fe6 4f22`):
  - `0`        30 KB  first-read program; loads INTRO.BIN. Contains "GFS_SBL Version 2.10 1996-02-01" (Sega SBL CD library).
  - INTRO.BIN  30 KB  loads CDCON.BIN then LORT.BIN.
  - CDCON.BIN  44 KB  CD/backup-RAM glue (BUP_* strings), dir "SCORSAT".
  - LORT.BIN   1 MB   THE GAME. Starts with `mov.l @(pc),r0; jmp r0` -> 0x06005E8A. ~1700 real strings.
- Engine loads struct layouts + symbol tables from TEXT at runtime: string "d:\wincode\h\maineng.asc", "SYMBOLBLK",
  "FATAL ERROR: unable to find struct", "structure not same size as struct %s".
  `SCORSAT/GAME/WINCODE/H/MAINENG.ASC` (1460 lines) ships on disc: constants (name value) then struct fields
  (name offset, size, typecode[, typename]). Struct families: seqptrs, BallR*, ColiR*, World*, Camera*, Alloc*,
  Impact*, BigFile*, Depack*, PathR*, radar*, Bike*, Trigger*, DrawP*... => free Ghidra struct definitions.
- Shared codebase with PC confirmed: LORT.BIN contains "temp:\scordos.bin", "c:\Games\Scorcher\", "l:\scor1.bin".
  Dev paths use drive letters l:, t:, d:, temp:. Danish identifiers (FJENDE=enemy, POKAL=trophy, gnister=sparks, vildt).
- Data formats: .MEG "MEGAFILE" archives (levels, title, options), .R0V (2D screens, "r0v 256 no sat support"),
  .FMZ/.FZP (raybobs = sprites: smoke, radar, boost, counters), .BLK/.MAP/.PAL (VDP2 backgrounds, 768-byte pal),
  .MUS (sequenced music), .JOY (recorded input, LV5REC.JOY), .HMP, SIN16388/TAN16384 lookup tables.
  Debug: "FATAL STIVERT", "FATAL SVED", "FMZ type".

  Launchers: `Tools\ghidra.bat` (GUI), `Tools\ghidra-headless.bat` (analyzeHeadless with JAVA_HOME set).

## .MEG container -- CRACKED (tools/megextract.py, output in extracted/)
- Big-endian table of entries [u32 kind=16][u32 offset][u32 size][u32 entry_len][dev path], terminated by kind 0xFFFFFFFF;
  data follows, 4-byte padded. 14 archives, ~820 files. Dev paths keep the original tree (t:\scorsat\game\...).
- A level = setups/levN/levN.txt: "W<world> <id> <setnr> <f> <f> ... P<path> <n>" -- list of "worlds" (entities) + a path.
  Worlds are Danish-named: fjende=enemy, skilt=sign, ting=thing, bilstyring=car steering, pokal=trophy.
- Each world = worlds/<name>/<name>.wl3 (0x4C header + 16-byte name + chunk stream) and setups/levN/worlds/<name>/setN.bin
  (overrides). Chunk = 4-char tag, u16 len, u16 count. Tags are the _Code* constants in MAINENG.ASC:
  LOAD VALS STRN MATX SMAT ROUT RROU GPTR PPTR ALOC NAME MPOS ANIM. NAME chunks bind engine routines by name
  (30 distinct: enecon01, bikewire, rotback, scboard, spdcon, flashflare, bgexpl, bilstyring, moincam, initglobal,
  pickbost, pickjmp, camlir, camwpul, triggerwpul, objpul, lensflare, stpcon, redlight, setvisi, demoexit, insins,
  endseqcam, himain, ilogomain, smoke, wldpul, optionsmain, titlemain, insrestarts).
- Other per-level files: bigs/<world>.ld (load list into the BIGS/<WORLD>.N stream files), paths/rNpath.bin (track path),
  scordat/lvNrec.joy (demo input), lvNHi.hmp (hi-score?), v/c/lort.ddd (16-bit descending table), raybobs/*.fmz+.fzp (sprites).

## Ghidra state (2026-10-09)
- LORT.BIN layout: code 0x06004000-0x06024000 and 0x060C4000-0x06100000; 0x06024000-0x060C4000 is zero (BSS/data reserve).
  Strings/paths live in the upper code region. Entry `lort_entry` = 0x06005E8A.

## Routine name table in LORT.BIN -- FOUND, functions named (10-09; layout corrected 10-10)
- Table at 0x060FBF98 (file offset 0x0F7F98): 37 entries of 52 bytes = MAINENG `_ConRouts`: char ConRoutName[16] +
  RoutPtr[9] (index = _RJmpPreInit 0, Init 1, Frmupdate 2, Visible 3, Coli 4, Display 5, RateLoop 6, CloseDown 7;
  index 8 is a spare, always 0). 0 = slot unused. (Until 10-10 the script read name[20] + 8 pointers, so every
  slot label was off by one and the four routines with a PreInit pointer -- system, insins, rotback, scboard -- were missed.)
- scordos / SCORWIN: 45 entries each (scordos table 0xa8704..); LORT lacks acidsfx barrel0 fire0 freecam install
  kransfx pumpsfx smoke speed0 (barrel0 missing in LORT = Saturn has no barrel routine). bjarnesfx exists in no build.
- ghidra/scripts/NameRoutineTable.java scans the program for such entries, labels each `rout_<name>`, names the slot
  functions `<name>_<slot>` (e.g. bilstyring_Init, titlemain_Display); re-runnable, replaces old slot names. Run:
  ghidra-headless.bat <proj> Scorcher -process <prog> -noanalysis -scriptPath ghidra/scripts -postScript NameRoutineTable.java
  10-10 re-run: scordos 45 entries / 101 names / 12 functions created, SCORWIN same, LORT 37 / 89 / 5.
- Shared addresses: pickcon01/barrel0 share Init+RateLoop; spdcon/stpcon share Init+Frmupdate (second name becomes a label).
- Game flow guess from names: ilogomain -> titlemain -> optionsmain/himain(hi-score) -> demosel -> world (wldpul) with
  bilstyring (car steering), enecon01 (enemy control), camwpul/camlir cameras, endseqcam.

## Watcom calling convention in Ghidra (10-09)
- ghidra/x86watcom.cspec = __watcall (default): args EAX, EDX, EBX, ECX then stack, callee pops (extrapop unknown -> read from ret n),
  EDX/EBX/ECX killed, ESI/EDI/EBP preserved, result EAX / EDX:EAX / ST0; plus __stdcall (Win32 imports) and __cdecl.
  Verified against the Open Watcom C/C++ User's Guide (32-bit register-based calling conventions chapter).
- Installed by copying it into Tools\ghidra\ghidra_12.1.4_PUBLIC\Ghidra\Processors\x86\data\languages\ and adding
  <compiler name="Open Watcom" spec="x86watcom.cspec" id="watcom"/> to x86.ldefs under x86:LE:32:default.
  Re-do both after a Ghidra upgrade. Import PC programs with: -processor x86:LE:32:default -cspec watcom
- SCORWIN.EXE and scordos.bin were re-imported (-overwrite) with it; structs + routine names re-applied.
  Verified (ghidra/checkconv.log, scripts/CheckConvention.java): compiler spec id "watcom", default proto __watcall, decompiled
  signatures take register args. Function counts after the reimport: SCORWIN 336 (was 380), scordos 501 (was 552) - discovery
  is still thin, so an x86 prologue/function-start scanner is the next step. Logs: ghidra/import_scorwin_watcom.log, import_scordos_watcom.log.

## Watcom function-start scanner (10-09)
- ghidra/scripts/FindWatcomFunctions.java: candidates = bytes after each function end (needs a Watcom prologue: saved-reg
  pushes, push ebp/mov ebp,esp, sub esp,n, mov eax,n/call __CHK) + any code address referenced by a 4-byte pointer in
  memory (loose opcode check). Each is disassembled + made a function, kept only if all flows land on instructions and the
  body reaches RET; up to 8 passes with analyzeChanges between. Run: ghidra-headless ... -process <prog> -postScript
  FindWatcomFunctions.java -postScript ScorcherStats.java (logs ghidra/findwatcom_*.log).
- Results: SCORWIN.EXE 336 -> 998 functions (28 KB -> 89 KB of ~231 KB code); scordos.bin 501 -> 1852 (71 KB -> 178 KB
  of ~335 KB code). The flat scordos image was one RWX block, so 113 false functions landed in data; ScordosSetup.java now
  splits the block per LE object with exec/write flags from the map (obj1-4 code, obj5-6 data) and CleanDataFunctions.java
  removed them (clean_scordos.log). Re-run the scanner after any reimport (ScordosSetup runs as pre-script, so it is safe now).
- Known gap: ~10 candidates fail with "body contains referring thunk" (Ghidra made a jmp stub inside them); fix by hand
  or by deleting the thunk first. Next: name the Watcom runtime (clib) functions, decode .wl3 chunks.

## Watcom runtime names (10-09)
- tools/omfsig.py matches Open Watcom OMF library code (fixup bytes wildcarded; full body, then 32/24/16-byte prefixes)
  against SCORWIN.EXE (PE sections) or flat:0x10000:pc/flat/scordos.bin, then follows the matched functions' fixups to
  name callees (self-relative call -> EXTDEF) and referenced data (absolute 32-bit -> EXTDEF). Output ghidra/sig_<prog>.txt,
  applied by ghidra/scripts/ApplyNames.java (<prog> -noanalysis -postScript ApplyNames.java <sig file>); names are IMPORTED
  source, with a plate comment naming the library module + confidence.
- Libraries (Sybase Open Watcom Public License, extracted from the installers, only lib386 kept):
  Tools\openwatcom19-dl\ow19\lib386 (1.9, closest to the 1996 Watcom 10.x runtime) and Tools\openwatcom-dl\ow\lib386 (v2).
  Use nt\clib3r.lib + math387r.lib + plib3r.lib for SCORWIN, dos\clib3r.lib + math387r.lib + plib3r.lib for scordos.
- Result: SCORWIN 31 runtime functions + 4 data labels; scordos 61 functions + 15 data labels. The runtime is small: the
  compiler inlined memcpy/strlen (all 27 rep-movsd sites are struct copies), so most of the clib is simply not linked in.
  Runtime lives at ~0x433000-0x4397ff in SCORWIN and ~0x4a000-0x61000 in scordos (plus DOS/4GW glue, FPE handlers).
- Not matched: ___Alphabet (data table in a CODE segment), __NTMain (prefix hit inside another function). The remaining
  unnamed functions in those ranges are mostly the Watcom 10.x versions of printf internals and startup; name by hand
  while reading, or by comparing with the Open Watcom source for the same module.

## Cross-build function alignment (10-09)
- Pipeline: `ghidra\scripts\ExportFuncInfo.java` (per program: `F addr name source size`, `C callee`, `S string`; strings
  are found directly, one hop through a pointer, or in undefined literal-pool slots) -> `tools\xalign.py` (votes:
  equal user/imported names 3, string literal referenced by exactly one function in both builds 2, call-order
  propagation 1; cross-compiler pairs need 2 votes; one-to-one by best vote, ties rejected; union-find groups) ->
  `ghidra\scripts\ApplyNames.java` with `ghidra\xalign_<prog>.txt` (plate comment "Cross-build match ...").
- Result: 203 groups (76 three-way, 126 two-way, 1 conflicting skipped). Applied 16 names to LORT.BIN, 99 to
  scordos.bin, 95 to SCORWIN.EXE. Names: existing user/imported name of any member, else `s_<string slug>_<scordos addr>`,
  else `fn_<scordos addr>` (SCORWIN addr when scordos has no member). Open the plate comment to see counterparts.
- LORT (Saturn) only got 16 because GCC and Watcom emit different call order and the Saturn build strips most strings.
  Re-run after naming more functions by hand: names propagate to the other builds on the next pass.
- Re-run: export the three TSVs with `-readOnly -postScript ExportFuncInfo.java <out.tsv>`, then
  `py -3.13 tools\xalign.py ghidra scordos=... SCORWIN=... LORT=...`, then ApplyNames per program (one job at a time).

## Watcom function bodies were truncated at __CHK (fixed 10-10)
- Ghidra had flagged the Watcom stack-check stub (`xchg eax,[esp+4]; call check; mov eax,[esp+4]; ret 4`) and the
  byte-swap helper FUN_000408b5 as non-returning, so every function was cut at its first `call __CHK`; the code after
  the call stayed undisassembled or became a separate "fragment" function (FindWatcomFunctions' loose end+1 mode made
  those). Coverage before the fix: scordos 178 KB of ~335 KB code, SCORWIN 90 KB of ~231 KB.
- `ghidra\scripts\FixNoReturn.java` cleared the flags but its regrow (Ghidra's CreateFunctionCmd.getFunctionBody)
  follows jumps into neighbours and deleted 83/27 real functions. Do not reuse it. `ghidra\restore_*.txt` list them.
- `ghidra\scripts\RepairBodies.java` is the correct tool: clears stale CALL_RETURN flow overrides on calls to
  returning functions, disassembles addr+len after such calls (Instruction.getFallThrough() stays null there),
  merges fragments whose only entry is fall-through from the previous instruction, grows bodies without crossing
  other functions, creates functions for orphan code runs. Run it until "merged 0 / grown 0" (5 passes each).
  Final: scordos 2289 functions, 337.2 KB; SCORWIN 1235 functions, 208.8 KB (~22 KB of SCORWIN is still fragments
  that share tails with neighbours; harmless). Re-run: `-process <prog> -noanalysis -postScript RepairBodies.java`.
- Alignment re-run afterwards: 449 groups (was 203), applied scordos 247, SCORWIN 266, LORT 42 names.
- 10-10 after the routine-slot fix: 465 groups (group sizes 2:334 3:129 4:2), +5/+4/+2 names.

## .wl3 chunk loader (scordos, hand-named 10-10; names carried to SCORWIN/LORT by xalign)
- `ghidra\hand_scordos.txt` (ApplyNames "hand" mode = USER_DEFINED names, plate "Hand-named: ...").
- Chunk = { u32 tag; u16 len; u16 count; data }, big-endian on disk even on PC. `wl3_SwapChunkList` (0x1eee0)
  byte-swaps a chunk list in place, dispatching on the tag (ALOC ANIM GPTR LOAD MATX MPOS NAME PPTR ROUT RROU SMAT
  STRN VALS); ROUT/RROU recurse via `wl3_SwapChunk`. Unknown tag -> `dbg_Printf` + `dbg_Pause`.
- Binding: `wl3_BindRoutines` (0x1cd97) walks the ROUT list, matches each NAME against the routine table
  (DAT_000a64a0 hash of the 52-byte rout_ entries: char name[20] + 8 slot pointers; flags |4 if Visible, |0x80 if Display) with `stricmp`, allocates WROUT (world) or OROUT (object)
  instances, then `wl3_BindWorldRoutine` / `wl3_BindObjectRoutine` apply VALS (2/4-byte pokes), STRN (string
  pointers), GPTR/PPTR (pointer fixups; PPTR also records WebLink nodes), LOAD (file names -> handles). Pointer
  targets come from `wl3_ResolvePtrRef`: kind 0..3 = none / world / object / routine param, then an offset chain.
- `dbg_Printf` (0x3f539) prints to the 200-column on-screen text grid and, in a debug mode, appends to
  c:\3dzwork\outlort.txt / outlort.err (the devs' work dir; "lort" = the Saturn binary name LORT.BIN).

## Developer research (10-09; no tools or source found)
- Zyrinx = Copenhagen Amiga demoscene people (Sub-Terrania team: David Guldbrandsen, Karsten Hvidberg, Jens Bo
  Albretsen, Thomas Risager); Scorcher grew from a 1995 32X tech demo moved to Saturn hardware; Scavenger funded
  voxel/NURBS/lightmap R&D. Red Zone beta is public domain; no Scorcher source or tool release exists, and no
  .meg/.wl3/LORT.BIN RE community. Sources: segasaturnshiro.com Scavenger article + Christian Laursen interview
  (video), segaretro.org/Scorcher, Wikipedia Zyrinx/Scorcher/Sub-Terrania, segaretro.org/Red_Zone, wiki.osdev.org/Watcom.

## Routine table read (10-10) -- all 83 scordos slot functions decompiled and read
Dump: ghidra/decomp_routines_scordos.c (DumpDecomp of every slot function). Which worlds bind which routine, with the
VALS/STRN parameters they poke into the instance block: `py -3.13 -I tools/wl3rout.py pc/extracted/TAGDEN/scorpc/worlds [name]`.

### Slot layout -- CONFIRMED and fixed (10-10)
MAINENG.ASC: `_ConRouts` = ConRoutName[16] + RoutPtr[9] at +16, `routdat` (28 bytes = the WROUT/OROUT instance:
RoutType, RoutPtr -> _ConRouts, WorldRAMHeaderPtr, WRamPtr, ObjRAMHeaderPtr, ORamPtr, nxtroutlink) and
`WorldRAMHeader_RamWorldRout` at +184 (0xb8) = list head the binder appends to. Index = _RJmp* number. Checks: index 8
is 0 in all 45+37 entries; the binder sets `_RAMWBColiRoutOn` (4) when RoutPtr[4] = Coli is set, 0x80 when [6] = RateLoop.
Use per index 0..8: scordos [2,28,9,11,3,9,8,19,0], LORT [1,26,8,5,3,8,8,17,0]. Meaning (from reading the functions):
  PreInit    one-shot before load: only system (config defaults) and insins/rotback/scboard (sprite/backdrop loads)
  Init       alloc / load / setup of the instance
  Frmupdate  every frame, always: path step (wldpul), spdcon countdown, freecam keys, demoexit check, endseqcam
  Visible    every frame while the world is in view: pickups spin+test, smoke/fire emit, anim, objpul, pumpsfx
  Coli       on contact with the hero body: spdcon/stpcon pads, bgexpl
  Display    per-frame draw/tick: all menu screens, redlight/lensflare/flashflare sprite emitters
  RateLoop   fixed-rate physics step: bilstyring, enecon01, pickcon01/barrel0, moincam/camwpul/camlir cameras
  CloseDown  frees everything (fn_40108 = free)
Names in Ghidra now follow this (NameRoutineTable.java re-run on all three programs; xalign re-run: 465 groups).
Side note: `FindSlotCalls.java` (lists CALL [reg+0x14..0x30]) found only sound-driver vtable calls; the engine loop
reaches the slots through a register, so the dispatcher is still to be located (search for readers of world+0xb8).

### What each routine does (slot names as now applied)
World movers (instance = WROUT block, parameters from the .wl3 VALS chunk)
- wldpul: moves its world along the MPOS point list; flags 1 ping-pong, 4 sine ease-in/out, 0x20 raw speed; writes
  position, velocity and matrix (fn_22718 lerps matrices). Base for camwpul/camlir (they call wldpul_Init/Frmupdate).
- trainwpul / triggerwpul: wldpul started when the hero's track position passes wrout+4..+8 (train 260..280 in r2trai03,
  trigger 330..335 in bjarne01), +0x48 speed, +0x3c end. trainwpul adds the train sound once (fn_23631(0x41,0x7d), DAT_000a6578).
- objpul: object-level mover (orout +0x28 accumulator, +0x2c speed, +0x30 index, +0x34 count, +0x38 flags); r3back01 x4.
- anim: vertex animation. Init finds the ANIM chunk in the world header, copies frame 0 into the mesh, builds a frame-
  pointer table (doubled for ping-pong, flag 1); +0xc phase 16.16, +4 frame count<<16, +8 vertex count. Frame step
  lerps between frame n and n+1 (fn_12030c fixed mul) then FUN_00021fd1 commits the mesh. Not named by any PC .wl3.
- spinn0: object matrix from the world's rotation angles each frame (fn_10989). bikewire: wireframe bike object, identity
  matrix scaled 0x10000000.
Pickups and effects
- pickjmp / pickbost: Init stores a spin phase seed (DAT_000c0bc4 / DAT_000c0bc8, +1000 per pickup; NOT a score) and
  hides the "jexpl"/"bexpl" explosion object (object flag 4 = hidden). Per frame: spin by frame delta*0x20, fn_2a508 =
  car-contact test; on pickup the car's jump (+0xd8) or boost (+0xdc) meter += 0x2000 capped 0x10000, a sprite is emitted
  (s_extram_18f02, sprite table DAT_000c0c18+0x84, callback FUN_0002a73c), the explosion object is unhidden and cloned
  (s_CPYOBJINS_183c8), sound fn_3b42b(0x230|0x234,0x7c), world flag |1 (dead), FUN_0002a5c1 drops the routine.
- pickopned: bobbing opened pickup. speed0: speed-boost pickup, adds impulse to the hero then removes itself.
- pickcon01 / barrel0: physically simulated prop: fn_238ca, fn_24563 x2 (gravity +0x1200 on wrout+0x10), fn_24699,
  fn_246c5, fn_26aef ground test from the track data (world+0xa0 +0x10/+8), fn_2597a, fn_2390f. Instance 0x178 like a car.
- smoke / fire0 / redlight / lensflare / flashflare: emit sprites or particles at MPOS points when within 0x1f400 of the
  camera (fn_107ed range test); fire0 transforms the point by the object matrix and emits with fn_234c5; lensflare
  projects 5 flare sprites toward screen centre.
- bgexpl: on hero contact when the hero is fast (speed > 0x73a0) copies the hero velocity into its own body, plays
  fn_23631(0x1f,0x78), removes itself unless flag 0x10, FUN_0001eb9b = explode.
- pumpsfx / kransfx / acidsfx: s_lort_d_2a79f(obj, sound, f0,f1,f2,f3): plays the sound (0x6e pump/crane, 0x73 acid)
  while the object's animation frame is in the range; acid tries a second range. Danish names: kran = crane.
- spdcon / stpcon (r?pspd / r?pstp worlds, "speed pad" / "stop pad"): Init hides every object whose name does not start
  with '['. Frame step counts down (+0 period, default 200) and shows the pad objects one after another (chevron
  sequence); +0xe set by a sibling = remove self. On hero contact: a '['-named object plays 0x23d/0x24a, sets +0xc and
  marks every sibling in dictionary +0x10 done; any other object plays 0x21c/0x2f and pushes the hero body 1/8 of the
  way toward the next pad's MPOS point (stpcon zeroes the velocity instead) and bumps dictionary entry 7 (+0x2c += 5,
  +0x30 = wrout+0x14 strength 20/80/8/16); the touched object is hidden.
Cars
- bilstyring (Danish "car steering"): the player car. Init: DAT_000c0cf8 = this world, physics 64000/500, wrout+0xf8
  speed 0x10000, +0xd8 0xa000. Rate step: fn_238ca, FUN_0002779a, FUN_00024bc1, fn_24563, FUN_00029c17, fn_24699,
  fn_246c5, fn_26aef, fn_2597a, FUN_00027064, FUN_00026dbd, FUN_00026e65, fn_23f2b/23f6b/23feb, fn_2390f (steering,
  suspension, ground, collision, damage). Worlds: ting (every level), lort.ddd via STRN.
- enecon01: enemy car (fjende1-5, fjende = enemy). Init physics 64000/0x96, path fn_28366; rate step paces by lane,
  target speed (6-lane)*2000+100000, dies on damage counters.
- initglobal: loads the two track data files, track length DAT_000c0ccc. insrestarts: builds the STMARKS restart table
  from MPOS points (r1rest..r6rest).
Cameras (camera state block PTR_DAT_000a86d4: +4 eye, +0x10 target, +0x1c matrix; fn_379f5 = look-at, fn_37654 = commit,
DAT_000c0b80 = camera world; CAMRAM 0x11c at PTR_DAT_000a64b8)
- moincam: main chase camera. Allocs CAMRAM + MOVERRAM (0x78, chase parameters at +0x44..+0x74). Looks up the hero by
  name (dictionary +0x84/+0x88), fn_39499 follow, fn_3860d/fn_389d6/fn_39261 placement; when the hero is dead
  (DAT_000c0cd4) orbits out over DAT_000c0c10 frames, blending direction with fn_102da/fn_12030c.
- camwpul: path camera: wldpul between hero track positions +4..+8, smoothed (accel 0x2000, damping 0x6000, snap when
  > 0x138800 away); flags at wrout+0xb8: 1 look at hero, 2 hide the hero (world flag 1), 4 sets CAMRAM+0xec, 8 one-shot.
  In the demo (+4 == 0) it runs on wall time (frame/0x21 < 60/90).
- camlir: same path camera but always active and aimed at a named world (+0x78); flag 8 = remove self.
- freecam: debug camera. Keys DAT_000bf988: +0xcb/+0xcd left/right, +200/+0xd0 up/down, +0x1d / +0x7f modifiers switch
  between move (32000/frame) and rotate (0x147); prints the first world name with dbg_Printf.
- endseqcam: end-sequence camera orbiting the MPOS points with sine motion over time; emits the sprites at
  DAT_000c0c18+0xe4 / +0x150 (title cards), fire (+0x1c) -> "title". DAT_000a86d8 = -1 means no trophy.
- setvisi: shows or hides the "pokal" (trophy) world depending on DAT_000a6630 (game finished).
Screens (shared: DRAWSCR 0x96000 at DAT_000c5c44, dirty rows DAT_000c5c48[640], s_lort_20451 clears rows, fn_33dd4
presents, GREMAP palette DAT_000c5fc4, pictures loaded with s_error_loading_file_s_4027d + s_R0V_pic_unknown_type_421830,
blits fn_2f120 / fn_2f3bc (keyed) / s_REMRGB_2f639 / fn_2f1d9; fn_17b0b ends the current level, fn_17875(name) starts one)
- ilogomain: SCAVLOGO, ZYRINX96, SCORLOGO 2 s each (frame time / 2000) then "title".
- titlemain: title + level select + config. State "TITLESEL" 0x54 bytes at DAT_000c5fcc: [9] frame, [10] page, [0xb]
  item, [0xc]/[0xd] key repeat, [0x10] previous item, [0x11]/[0x12] key latches. Pages PTR_DAT_000a82fc (items 0x18
  bytes: x, y, left action, right action, ..., -1 ends), page pictures PTR_DAT_000a8300, page handler PTR_FUN_000a8304
  called with 1 (draw current), 2 (tick), 3 (selection changed). Keys DAT_000bf988: +0xd0 down, +200 up, +0x1c fire,
  +0xe/+1 back. Idle 30 s -> attract demos PTR_s_lev5d1 (4 names). Cheat strings FUN_00021530: "XXXYZZZYYYZXX" -> endseq,
  "XYXZY" -> DAT_000a668c |= 1.
- optionsmain: in-game options, same machine on PTR_DAT_000a8648 tables, wraps the selection.
- himain: results / hi-score. Lap times DAT_000c5c00[laps DAT_000a6660]; best-lap table DAT_000c5c34 (0x3c per level),
  best-total DAT_000c5c30, overall DAT_000c5c2c; FUN_0003519f inserts -> DAT_000c6080/84/88 entry slots; name entry
  FUN_00035546 into DAT_000a6668; names matching DAT_000a218d / DAT_000a2191 set DAT_000a668c |= 2 / |= 1. Then
  campaign (DAT_000a6650 == 1 && DAT_000a665c == 1): next level PTR_DAT_000a6694[DAT_000a6658], level 6 -> "endseq";
  otherwise "title".
- demosel: demo build level pick (DemoLev1 / DemoLev5, DAT_000c6094), fire -> level name at DAT_000a25a2/a25a7.
  demoexit: ends the demo level and returns to "title" when the hero passes track position (DAT_000bf80c+300)>>8.
- install: the installer. Default path "c:\Games\Scorcher\" (DAT_000c60d0), states 0 path entry (checks drive, writes
  test.bin, needs free space from s_FBUF_3719e), 1 copying (FUN_00036f71, progress /0x1a8), 2 invalid, 3 complete
  (writes config via FUN_0003f3df), 4 out of space.

Four routines found only after the layout fix (10-10, dump: scratchpad newrout.c -> read):
- system: PreInit copies the 0x26-dword config defaults DAT_000a65fc -> DAT_000c0c1c and sets the palette (fn_3d902);
  Init / Frmupdate (unless the level is "endseq") (re)allocate SCRBUF DAT_000bf84c = width DAT_000c7e2c x height
  DAT_000c7e30 x bytes/pixel (DAT_000c7e42>>16) when the resolution changes; CloseDown writes the 0x98-byte config back
  to the file named at DAT_000a65f8 ("Error creating %s"). Engine-level service routine, not a world routine.
- insins: PreInit allocates the sprite table DAT_000c0c18 (0x168) and loads l:
aybobslenzf, endtext, txt_winner
  (fn_1dad7 FMZ+FZP pairs) + KFRM; CloseDown frees them. "ins" = instance sprites for the HUD/end text.
- rotback: the "back" world of most levels = backdrop picture (BGPIC). PreInit (re)loads it when the backdrop options
  DAT_000a6670/DAT_000a6674 changed or on a level change; Display draws it into SCRBUF in 1/2/4 bytes per pixel;
  CloseDown frees four buffers.
- scboard: the in-race scoreboard / HUD on the camera worlds. Frmupdate draws lap, position, time digits and the
  power-up icons with s_extram into DAT_000c0c18 sprites, counts the cars ahead from the dictionary of cars
  (fn_112f4/fn_1131c), handles the race-over timers (-> "title" / "hisetup" screens), pause ("options"), lap split
  bonus (DAT_000c5bfc[lap]); PreInit sets up the hi-score record block in mode DAT_000bf7fc==2.
Name used by a PC world but absent from every table: bjarnesfx (bjarne01) -- stricmp fails, silently unbound.
Not bound by any PC .wl3 (bound from code or Saturn-only): wldpul camwpul camlir freecam anim setvisi endseqcam demosel
demoexit initglobal redlight lensflare flashflare fire0 smoke speed0 spinn0 pickcon01 pickopned.
Helpers identified: fn_1d7c8 remove routine/object, fn_40108 free, s_extram_18f02 emit sprite, fn_107ed in-range test,
fn_12030c fixed mul, fn_10170 sine, fn_10a36 random, fn_22718 matrix lerp, fn_23631 / fn_3b42b play sound, fn_109e5
matrix from angles, fn_1026b vector length, fn_112f4+fn_1131c dictionary lookup/iterate.

## Engine dispatch and main loop -- FOUND, hand-named (10-10)

All addresses are scordos (flat image). SCORWIN names carried over by xalign where the group matched
(rout_CallSlot 0x4024b2, eng_StartLevel 0x4088a5, eng_Frame 0x408e9e); LORT only got 2 from this batch.
Names live in `ghidra/hand_scordos.txt` (format `0x<addr> func <name> hand <comment>`, applied with ApplyNames.java).

### Routine dispatch
- `rout_CallSlot` 0x114b2 (routdat list, slot): walks the world's routdat list (next at +0x18) and calls
  `_ConRouts.RoutPtr[slot]` = `[RoutPtr + 0x10 + 4*slot]` with (WorldRAMHeader, WRam, ObjRAMHeader, ORam)
  = routdat +8,+0xc,+0x10,+0x14. Counts calls in DAT_000bf9e0. This is the proof of the slot layout.
- `rout_CallSystem` 0x1138e (slot): looks up the "system" routine in the routine hash DAT_000a64a0 and calls
  its slot with (0,0,0,0).
- `world_CallSlotAll` 0x133f4 (slot): runs a slot over every world in the engine context list (ctx+0x20,
  next +0x10). The Frmupdate (2) pass also calls Visible (3) when ConBits & 0x10, with
  `world_SaveOldPosMatrix` 0x132ce before and `world_UpdateMovedFlags` 0x13319 after, unless ConBits & 0x80
  (= routine has a RateLoop, which already did that). The RateLoop (6) pass uses the fixed time ctx+0x138
  (`world_RateLoopAll` 0x13483, per world FUN_00013375 first). `world_CallSlotAllPlain` 0x134c6 = no extras.
- `car_CollideStep` 0x26aef: car movement substeps; on hitting a world with ConBits & 4 it calls that
  world's Coli (4) routines directly (fn_2390f before, fn_238ca after).

### Frame and level flow
- `eng_Frame` 0x17e99: frame counter ctx+0x30, reseed; for each pending tick (DAT_000bf7d4..d8)
  ctx+0x138 += 0x21 (33 ms, fixed rate ~30 Hz) and RateLoop all worlds; then Frmupdate all worlds; then
  `eng_RenderFrame` 0x17d99 if there is a camera world (DAT_000c0b80) else Display (5) only; finally
  FUN_0001da50 and a stack guard check (DAT_000bf7f4+0x2000 == DAT_000bf7f8, else fn_3fb31 fatal).
  Callers: FUN_00020cef, __CHK 0x20d1c, caseD_2 0x20e1f (per-bpp cases of the level loop).
- `eng_LevelLoop` 0x211f2: while the level stack DAT_000bf820 is non-empty -> `eng_PopLevelStack` 0x17c03
  (state DAT_000bf994 = 2, allocs the 0x2000 STACK with 0x55555555 guard, calls WORLDLINK(0));
  if a level name is pending (DAT_000bf824) -> `eng_StartLevel` 0x178a5 (copies name into ctx, ATAB3 hash,
  allocs, system Init, Init (1) all worlds, system Frmupdate, PreInit pass, state 3); then switches on bytes
  per pixel (DAT_000c7e42 >> 16) to the screen converter case that runs eng_Frame.
- `eng_Init` 0x21389 (ilogos): hash tables, system PreInit (0), state 4. `eng_CloseDown` 0x21468: system
  CloseDown (7), frees.
- `eng_SetVideoMode` 0x20e7d (caller FUN_0003dce9): reallocs SCRBUF; on bpp change re-runs PreInit (0) on all
  worlds with DAT_000bf7fc = 1. `wl3_BindAndPreInit` 0x1d62f runs PreInit with DAT_000bf7fc = 2 after a
  fresh load, so DAT_000bf7fc tells a PreInit *why* it is being called (1 = mode change, 2 = load).
  FUN_0001d511 (SMAT chunk -> sub-worlds) also PreInits each sub-world.
- `world_Free` 0x1d7f4: CloseDown (7) for the world's routines, unlink from ctx+0x20, free objects, palettes,
  link records, routdat list (fn_11410).

### Routine order of a frame (as the engine sees it)
PreInit (load / mode change) -> Init (level start) -> [RateLoop x n at 33 ms -> Frmupdate (+Visible)
-> Display / render] per frame -> CloseDown (level end / world free). Coli only from car collision.

Alignment after the hand names: 465 groups (2:334 3:130 4:1), 1 conflicting, applied scordos 1 / SCORWIN 11 / LORT 2.
Next candidates: FUN_00013375 (per-world RateLoop prep), FUN_0001da50 (post-frame), the bpp screen converters
FUN_00020cef / caseD_*, and the input/car routines (bilstyring) that feed car_CollideStep.

## Car physics, frame timer, video flip -- hand-named (10-10, second pass)

All in `ghidra\hand_scordos.txt` (115 names applied to scordos; xalign carried 38 to SCORWIN, 9 to LORT;
466 groups, 3 conflicting). Decompiles used: scratchpad `cand.c`, `car.c`, `pre.c` (not kept).

### Frame timer (eng_FrameTimer 0x17f53, called first in every level-loop iteration)
- Waits until >= 11 ms since the last frame (DAT_000bf7c8), calling sys_Idle(0) 0x3d1dc meanwhile (frame cap ~90 Hz).
- dt clamped to 4..500 ms; smoothed frame time DAT_000bf7c4 += (dt - s) >> 3; a spike > 2*s is clamped to s.
- ctx = DAT_000bf80c: +0x130 game ms, +0x134 previous ms, +0x138 ms consumed by RateLoop (eng_Frame adds 0x21 per tick),
  +0x12c / +0x128 = ms*256/33 (tick time, 8.8). DAT_000c0b84 = dt, DAT_000a648c = dt/33 as 16.16 (frame length in ticks).
- DAT_000bf7d8 = RateLoop ticks to run this frame = (ms/33 + 1) - consumed/33. DAT_000bf7d4 = current tick index inside
  eng_Frame; the car code tests `DAT_bf7d4 == 0` (first tick) and `DAT_bf7d8 - 1 == DAT_bf7d4` (last tick) for once-per-frame work.

### Level-loop iterations (the "bpp switch" in eng_LevelLoop)
- caseD_1 -> eng_LoopIter8bpp 0x20d4e: VGA 320x200 linear fast path eng_LoopIterVga320 0x20cef (DAT_000c7e28==1,
  DAT_000c7e1c==1, width DAT_000c7e2c==0x140) else eng_LoopIter8bppGeneric 0x20d17.
- caseD_2 -> eng_LoopIter16bpp 0x20e1f; caseD_3/4 (24/32 bpp) are empty = unsupported.
- Iteration order: mem_ResetMarks, eng_FrameTimer, vid_FlipDirtyRects (previous frame), eng_Frame, vid_ClearWorkBuffer,
  timing print (string "tid" = Danish time), vid_PageFlip. The 16bpp fast path flips before eng_Frame.
- vid_FlipDirtyRects 0x20d97: work buffer DAT_000bf84c (w DAT_000bf854, h DAT_000bf858) -> screen (A0000 in mode 13h,
  else vid_LockScreen 0x3df7c / vid_UnlockScreen 0x3e047) through the dirty-rect list DAT_000bf9e8 (vid_AddDirtyRect 0x20451,
  0x1c-byte nodes from mem_Alloc; whole screen = (0,0,0x10000,0x10000)); vid_BlitRect 0x2063d does the per-bpp conversion.
- vid_SetMode13h 0x3dce9 fills the mode globals from the 31-byte mode table DAT_000a90a2[DAT_000c7e74] and does int 10h/13h.
- mem_Alloc 0x402ef (&out, size, tag string, flags), mem_Free 0x40323, mem_PushMark 0x40398 (heap checkpoints with a name,
  30 x 0x14 at DAT_000d2070), mem_ResetMarks 0x403ec per frame.
- Fixed point helpers in the far code object: fixmul 0x12030c, fixmuldiv 0x1204a5; fixdot3 0x100d3, vec_Length 0x1026b (x87).
- Engine list helpers (guessed from use): list_Begin 0x112f4, list_Next 0x1131c, list_First 0x11361, list_Remove 0x1124f.
- world_FreeQueued 0x1da50 (post-frame) frees every world on the list DAT_000a649c.
- world_ObjSaveOldMatrix 0x13375: per routdat with ObjROMHeader.ObjControl & 0x40, sets ObjRAMHeader.ConBits |= 0x400 and
  copies ObjRAMExtHeader matrix/cen to OldMatrix/OldCen (same shape as world_SaveOldPosMatrix). Confirms RamWorldMatrix at
  WorldRAMHeader+0x5c and pos at +0x20.

### Car (bilstyring) per-tick step
bilstyring_RateLoop 0x29d71 -> DAT_000c0ce8==1 ? car_AIDriveStep 0x2a370 : car_PlayerDriveStep 0x29d9f (the tail of the
same function, Ghidra split it). enecon01_RateLoop (enemy cars) uses car_DriveStepAI 0x29eba directly, so player and
enemies share the physics; only steering input differs.

Order inside car_PlayerDriveStep (world = WorldRAMHeader, wram = routine RAM, shared = world+0x98 record):
1. car_PullSharedState 0x238ca: if shared flag byte set, take pos/vel from the shared record (restart/teleport).
2. car_Respawn 0x2779a: no track -> first restart point; else off-track timer wram+0x138 > 500 s or lap check -> nearest
   restart point behind the car (20-byte records: matrix ptr, pos xyz, progress). Returns 1 and the caller sets ConBits |= 2.
3. first tick only: car_EngineSoundMix 0x24bc1 (gear = speed*n/0x56b80, n<=25, fades DAT_000c0d64[] to DAT_000c0d00[],
   snd_SetVolume(0x14e+i)).
4. car_DampVelocity 0x24563: rotate vel into car space, scale (0.945, 0.992, 0.996), rotate back.
5. car_ReadKeys 0x29c17: 8 key states DAT_000bf988[DAT_000a6600..0a661c] -> car_ApplyControls 0x24cbc (1605 bytes, the
   steering/throttle code; not read yet). DAT_000c0cec != 0 disables input (steering wram+0x24 = 0).
6. gravity: wram+0x10 (vel y) += 0x1200 per 33 ms tick.
7. car_UpdateSpeed 0x24699 (wram+0x20 speed, +0x28 decel), car_Integrate 0x246c5 (pos += vel), car_CollideStep(..., 6).
8. last tick: car_UpdateSoundEmitter 0x23f2b, car_EmitSmoke 0x27498 (particle via s_extram_18f02, template DAT_000a7658).
9. car_GroundResponse 0x2597a (wram+0x38 on-ground, +0x3c air/ground counter, contact record at shared+0x28) ->
   car_ApplySurfaceNormal 0x254a0; car_UpdateTilt 0x27064 (body lean toward the track up vector track+0x2c..0x34);
   car_BuildBodyMatrix 0x26dbd (mat_BuildRotation 0x224c2 -> wram+0x88, 9 ints); car_BuildWorldMatrix 0x26e65
   (-> WorldRAMHeader+0x5c; its matrix helper 0x222f8 is still not a function in Ghidra).
10. last tick: car_UpdateTrackPos 0x23f6b (track_ProjectPos 0x23a52 -> wram+0xd0/+0xd2), car_UpdateLapCount 0x23feb
    (progress wram+0xf0, 16-sector index +0xec, sector jump >9 = lap +-1 in +0xe8, best +0xe4, wrong-way detection).
11. car_PushSharedState 0x2390f writes pos/vel back to the shared record.

AI variant (car_DriveStepAI): car_AISteer 0x28c0c (track_ProjectPos + car_AIComputeSteer 0x289b9 -> target angles) instead of
keys, heading-change counter wram+0x13a, and when countdown wram+0x128 <= 0 car_ForEachNearbyCar 0x28366 (walks the car
list DAT_000c0ce4) with a callback (FUN_00027f19 radius 0x1f400 or DAT_00027e74 radius 0x5dc00) = avoidance/ramming.
CORRECTED 10-10 (seventh pass): wram+0xf4 = following a recorded path, not crashed; car_PathStep 0x2a056 (ex car_CrashStep) follows it (s_got_joy_ptr_0_27bff next pos, fn_268cb collision,
car_PathSetSpin 0x284f6), car_PathLeave 0x2a028 (ex car_CrashEnd) clears it. DAT_000c5bfc==1 = pre-start freeze.

Car wram offsets seen: +0xc..+0x14 vel, +0x18 tilt, +0x1c smoke countdown, +0x20 speed, +0x24/+0x26 steering (shorts),
+0x28 decel, +0x38 on ground, +0x3c ground counter, +0x3e..+0x62 short angle sets (current/target/smoothed, car_ResetAngles
0x240ae fills them from the matrix), +0x64..+0x6c angular vel, +0x88 body matrix, +0xb8 ?, +0xd0/+0xd2 track angles,
+0xd8/+0xdc clamped minima, +0xe4 best lap, +0xe8 lap, +0xec sector, +0xf0 progress, +0xf4 on-path flag (was: crashed), +0x100, +0x128 AI
countdown, +0x130, +0x138 off-track timer, +0x13a heading-change count. Shared record (world+0x98): +0 flag, +4 pos,
+0x10 collision radius 64000, +0x14 mass 500 (set in bilstyring_Init), +0x18 vel, +0x24 track ROM ptr (+0x74 = has track), +0x28 contact record.

Next candidates: car_ApplyControls 0x24cbc (steering/throttle constants = the handling), car_CollideStep internals and the
Coli routines of the things it hits, car_AIComputeSteer 0x289b9, enecon01 (enemy control) RateLoop around car_DriveStepAI,
vid_BlitRect per-bpp loops, and the 0x222f8 / 0x111b5 / 0x120300 code that Ghidra has not made functions.

### Car handling: car_ApplyControls 0x24cbc and helpers (10-10, third pass)
124 hand names now; xalign 466 groups, SCORWIN 42 / LORT 9 (snd_Play carried to SCORWIN 0x43350a). Decompiles in scratchpad ctl.c / ctl2.c (not kept).

Key bindings DAT_000a6600..0a661c (scancodes, 0x80 = extended): keys[0] Up 0xc8 accelerate, [1] Down 0xd0 brake/reverse,
[2]/[3] Left/Right 0xcb/0xcd slide, [4]/[5] Left/Right again = steer (so one arrow does both), [6] Alt 0x38 nitro, [7] Ctrl 0x1d jump.
car_CameraKeys 0x246f2 reads raw key states: 0xf (Tab) toggles the camera FOV DAT_000a6574 0x31c7 <-> 0x4000 (eased 1/16 into
PTR_DAT_000a64b8+0x30), 0x7f toggles bit 0 of PTR_DAT_000a64b8[0xec].

Per tick (33 ms), wram offsets:
- speed = wram+0x20 signed by fixdot3(vel, forward shorts +0x4a/+0x4c/+0x4e); slip% = |old speed +0x134 - speed|*100/max.
- Race start: while game ms/33 < 100, boost timer +0x2c = 10, strength +0x30 = 200 -> engine +0x130 += 200*k + 20*k per tick.
- Nitro (Alt, energy +0xdc > 0): +0x170 = 1 (wheelspin), +0xdc -= 0x280, engine +0x130 += 3000 up to 0x1e848 (125000).
- Otherwise, when not airborne (+0x3c >= -9): speedRatio = fixmuldiv(speed, power short +0x13c, 0x1ce80) smoothed 2:1 when
  falling, kept in +0x164. Up: engine += car_AccelCurve 0x24af1 = ratio<0: 1500 | boost: min(timer,200)*20 | <61: (60-r)*83+1000
  | <126: (125-r)*6+250 | <201: 100 | else 0. Down: engine -= car_BrakeCurve 0x24b73 = forward: 1500 | r=-ratio <40:
  (40-r)*100+400 | <100: 200 | else 0. Neither: decay (slip+5, max 100)/200 and thrust = fixmuldiv(diff, 0x46, 100).
- Engine value +0x130 pulled toward the real speed outside a +-10000 deadband with gain (slip>>4 + 3)%, max 100%.
- Wheelspin +0x170 > 0: decrement, +0x24 (short) ++, snd_Play 0x3b42b (0x5a, vol 0x6e), car_EmitSpinParticle 0x27271
  (template DAT_000a7618, random velocity); when it ends and +0x22>>16 != 0: +0x24 = 0, snd_Play(0x5a, 0) stops it.
- Thrust x forward direction is added to vel +0xc..+0x14; Left/Right keys 2/3 add -+0x2800 x side vector +0x50..+0x54 (slide).
- Steer keys 4/5 (exclusive): steer = +-(+0x168 + 0x90), +0x168 += 4 per tick held, clamp 0x200; smoothed +0x16c = (old*7 +
  new*3)/10 -> car_ApplySteerYaw 0x247ba rotates all three short angle sets (+0x40, +0x4c, +0x58) about the up axis. Both
  released -> +0x168 = 0 (no return-to-centre spring; the yaw just stops).
- car_Jump 0x25339 (Ctrl): needs jump energy +0xd8 and the contact normal ((world+0x98)+0x28, +0x20..+0x28) facing the car up
  row (world matrix +0x68..+0x70), and (+0x34 != 0 or ground counter > -3). Held: counter +0x34 0..9, tick 0 snd_SetVolume(0x3c,
  0x78), vel -= up * DAT_000a7584[counter]*4 with the table 0.4, 0.2, 0.1 ... 0.0008 (16.16, halving), energy -= 0x2000 per tick.
  So a full jump is 10 ticks ~0.33 s with impulse ~0.8 total; car_Respawn refills +0xd8 >= 0xa000 (5 ticks) and +0xdc >= 0x2aaa.
- vec_ToLocal 0x10640 / vec_ToWorld 0x105bf: x87 rotate by the world matrix +0x5c (guess from car_DampVelocity).
- snd_Play 0x3b42b (sampleId, volume): volume 0 stops; sample record DAT_000c6874[(id&0xff)-0x1c][id>>8], channel table
  DAT_000c7d20[] (count DAT_000c7d1c), gated by DAT_000a9094 (sound enabled).

Next candidates: car_CollideStep internals and the Coli routines it calls, car_AIComputeSteer 0x289b9, enecon01 RateLoop,
vid_BlitRect per-bpp loops, FUN_0001036c / fn_10483 (angle-set rotate), the un-functioned code at 0x222f8 / 0x111b5 / 0x120300.

### Car collision: car_CollideStep 0x26aef and the contact record (10-10, fourth pass)
142 hand names; xalign SCORWIN 55 / LORT 10. Decompiles in scratchpad col.c / col2.c (not kept).
Correction: shared record +0x10 = collision radius (64000), +0x14 = mass (500); car-car impulses use the masses.

Contact record col = (world+0x98)+0x28, >= 0x84 bytes:
- +0 flags byte, +1 hit type byte (0 none, 1 solid, 6 another car, 0xff cleared), +2/+4 short = active ground contact
  slots (0, 1 or 2), +0xc hit face ptr, +0x10 hit world, +0x14..+0x1c hit point, +0x20..+0x28 hit normal, +0x2c..+0x34 other
  car pos, +0x38 hit fraction t (16.16).
- Contact slot A at +0x54, slot B at +0x6c, 0x18 bytes each: +0 type short, +2 flags byte, +4 world, +8 face, +0xc..+0x14 normal
  (so normals at +0x60 and +0x78, which car_GroundResponse reads).

car_CollideStep(world, wram, objList = level(world+0xa0)+0x10, carList = level+8, n = 6):
1. wram+0x38 = 0; with 1 slot col_ValidateContact1 0x26506, with 2 col_ValidateContacts2 0x2658e: drop slots whose world is
   gone (world_IsAlive 0x1c690 walks ctx+0x20) or whose face no longer contains the car (col_TestFace 0x262c0: point into
   object space, 3/4-vertex face from the mesh, FUN_00122ce5), re-settle on the survivor (col_ApplyContact 0x25fb6) or slide
   along the edge of two (col_SlideAlongEdge 0x2619d: cross of the two normals, push-out 0x100). Sets wram+0x38 = 1 if any holds.
2. Up to n substeps: col_Trace 0x268cb sweeps from the old pos (wram+0..+8) to the new world pos +0x20 with radius shared+0x10:
   objects via col_SegVsSphere 0x120c70 (ROM bounding sphere) then col_SegVsMesh 0x120d24; cars via col_SegVsSphereHit 0x120c9e
   (type 6, normal = -(other - hit point)); a hit car without ConBits & 0x10 counts as solid (type 1).
3. Hit world with ConBits & 4: push shared state, rout_CallSlot(world list +0xb8, Coli) with col+0x10 = this car, pull state,
   retrace if the hit world has flag bit 0 or the face has flag 4 (the Coli routine moved something).
4. Type 6: col_CarCarResponse 0x25ee3 -> phys_CollideSpheres 0x25bcf (centre-of-mass frame, restitution 0x6666 = 0.4,
   minimum separating push 100) on both velocities, the other car's shared flag = 0xff so it pulls the new vel next tick,
   snd_SetVolume(0x2f, 0x7c) crash sound. Other types: col_SurfaceResponse 0x266dd: vec_ReflectDamped 0x23b1c with the
   coefficients from col_GetResponseCoeffs 0x25f99 (0x3000 = 0.19 bounce, 0xfe00 = 0.992 and 0xf400 = 0.953 tangential), extra
   fixmul(0x13cc, 1-t) when landing from the air, wram+0x38 = 1, col_DropContactsFacing 0x260b1, store the face as slot A/B
   (with two already, replace the one less opposed to the velocity), two slots -> col_SlideAlongEdge.
5. pos = hit point + vel * (1 - t); old pos = hit point; loop. No hit on a substep -> pos = old pos (move completed).

Next candidates: the Coli routines of the things cars hit (grep routine table slot 4), car_AIComputeSteer 0x289b9, enecon01
RateLoop, col_SegVsMesh 0x120d24 / FUN_00122ce5 (mesh collision), vid_BlitRect per-bpp loops, FUN_0001036c / fn_10483,
the un-functioned code at 0x222f8 / 0x111b5 / 0x120300.

### Coli routines (10-10, fifth pass)
Only three routine-table entries have a Coli slot: spdcon, stpcon, bgexpl. 151 hand names; xalign SCORWIN 57 / LORT 10.
A Coli routine gets (world, wram, ObjRAMHeader, ORam) of the *hit* world; the car that hit it is col+0x10 of that world's
own contact record ((world+0x98)+0x28), written by car_CollideStep just before rout_CallSlot. The hit sub-object is the
car's col+0xc. Sub-objects: array world+0xc, count (world+8)->+0x10, each with flags byte +0xc (bit 4 = collidable, also the
"retrace" bit car_CollideStep checks) and +0x14 -> ROM header (+0x58 name string, +0x48>>16 index, +0x2c..+0x34 a local point).

- spdcon_Coli 0x39a7c (speed pads, "speed control"): a sub-object whose name starts with [ is the finish marker: sound 0x23d,
  wram+0xc = 1, siblings (list wram+0x10) disabled (+0xe = 1). A normal pad: sound 0x21c, and unless it is the last one the
  car is pulled toward the next pad: car shared flag = 1 (car_PullSharedState takes it) and shared vel += (next pad point in
  world space - car pos) / 8; the first record of global list 7 gets +0x2c += 5 and +0x30 = wram+0x14 (score/bonus, guess).
- stpcon_Coli 0x39bec (stop pads): same finish-marker logic with sound 0x24a; a normal pad plays 0x2f and zeroes the car's
  shared velocity (dead stop).
- spdcon_Init 0x39a3f sets the collidable bit on every non-[ sub-object; spdcon_Frmupdate 0x39cd9 is the sequencer: wram+0
  ticks per pad (default 200), +4 countdown 16.16 (-= DAT_000a648c per frame), +8 current pad, after the finish marker it
  re-lights or clears the pads one per countdown and re-enables the siblings; world flag bit 0 (hidden) while +0xe is set.
- bgexpl_Coli 0x2a465 (breakable scenery): if the car is faster than 0x73a0 the object takes the car's velocity (y clamped
  <= 0) and either world_QueueFree 0x1d7c8 (flag 8 + list DAT_000a649c) or, with world flag 0x10, explosion sound 0x1f +
  world_BreakApart 0x1eb9b (copies each non-collidable sub-object out as its own instance via s_CPYOBJINS_183c8, hides the
  original). FUN_0002a48c is a bogus function Ghidra started mid-instruction inside bgexpl_Coli; ignore it.
- vec_LocalToWorldPos 0x1075d = vec_ToWorld + world pos. stub_Nop 0x22d7a is an empty function.

Next candidates: car_AIComputeSteer 0x289b9, enecon01 RateLoop/Init (enemy cars; enecon01_Init calls world_QueueFree),
col_SegVsMesh 0x120d24 / FUN_00122ce5, s_CPYOBJINS_183c8 (object instancing), list 7 (what record spdcon scores into),
vid_BlitRect per-bpp loops, FUN_0001036c / fn_10483, un-functioned 0x222f8 / 0x111b5 / 0x120300.

### AI steering: car_AIComputeSteer 0x289b9 and helpers (10-10, sixth pass)
hand_scordos.txt now 160 names (xalign unchanged: SCORWIN 57, LORT 10). Decompiles: scratchpad ai.c / ai2.c / ai3.c (not kept).
- car_AISteer 0x28c0c (per tick, AI cars): track_ProjectPos(track, pos) -> (progress, lateral, height) ->
  car_AIComputeSteer; wram+0xd0 = progress>>16, wram+0xd2 = lateral>>16; then car_AIPostSteer 0x27269 -> stub_Nop_2a6cc
  (both empty: a hook that was compiled out). No track: stub only.
- car_AIComputeSteer 0x289b9, countdown wram+0x128 <= 0 (normal driving):
  1. car_AILookAhead 0x28624: segment table DAT_000c0cdc (count = *tbl>>16, 16-byte entries from tbl+4; byte +1 = segment
     flags). Two iterations of track_LaneCosts3 0x285af (three squared track_LaneCost 0x28588 samples at lane-1/lane/lane+1,
     64-byte segment stride): lateral += (left-right)/16, progress += 1.0 - |left-right|/16, so it looks less far ahead where
     the lane costs differ (turns). Returns lookProgress + 0x1000, target lateral and the current segment flags.
  2. track_PointAt 0x2394a (track, progress, lateral, height -> xyz; lateral scale 0x3e800; track_FindSegment 0x1e443 walks
     the 0x48-byte segment nodes of track+0x74 (+4 pos, +0x24 length, wrap DAT_000c0b94->DAT_000c0b98) and lerps with
     fn_1e60d; fn_1e885/fn_1eaa6 are the same spline helpers car_UpdateSoundEmitter uses) for the look-ahead point and the
     current point; target vector = difference.
  3. slope = fixmuldiv(|dy| (>=0x1000), 0x10000, |dx| (>=0x1000), 0x7000) passed to car_Jump; jumpKey = segment flag 0x80
     or a jump already running (wram+0x34); wram+0xd8 (jump energy) forced to 1 first, so AI cars always may jump.
  4. speed factor from the segment flags: base 0x8000; 0x20 -> 0xc000; 0x10 and 0x08 add 0x4000 each; 0x40 -> 0x1000 (slow
     section, overrides). thrust = fixmul(fixmul(car_AIThrottle(wram,1,0), factor), aiParam+4) (aiParam = the record whose
     +4 is a per-car speed multiplier: skill/difficulty, guess), min 0x2000; vec_Normalize(target, thrust>>2) is added to vel.
  Countdown running (after crash/avoidance): if ground counter wram+0x3c > -2, vel += forward dir * 0x2000.
- car_AIThrottle 0x28737 (wram, accel, brake) = engine model, returns engine value wram+0x130 minus old speed wram+0x134:
  signed speed = |vel| * sign(dot(vel, forward)); pct = |old speed - speed|*100/max(..). Airborne (ground counter < -9):
  engine -= (engine - speed)*(pct+5)%, return 70% (0x46/100) of (engine - speed). accel: index = speed*power(wram+0x13c
  short)/0x1ce80 into the curve table (count at [0], 16.16 entries after) -> engine += entry>>16 * 0x1e; brake: * -0xf; if
  |engine - speed| > 10000 pull engine back by (pct/8+3)%; no key: decay as airborne. Ghidra split the tail off as
  car_AIThrottle_tail 0x289a2 (fixmuldiv + store old speed).
- Segment flags (byte +1 of the 16-byte DAT_000c0cdc entries): 0x80 jump here, 0x40 slow, 0x20/0x10/0x08 speed tiers.
  These come from the level data (track segment table, loaded where DAT_000c0cdc is filled) -- not located yet.

Next candidates: where DAT_000c0cdc (segment table) and the AI param record (aiParam+4) are filled (grep callers writing

un-functioned 0x222f8 / 0x111b5 / 0x120300.

### Enemy cars: enecon01 + recorded paths (10-10, seventh pass)
hand_scordos.txt now 173 names; xalign SCORWIN 64, LORT 15 (466 groups, 2 conflicting). Decompiles: scratchpad ene.c / ene2.c.
BIG CORRECTION: wram+0xf4 is not a crash flag. It means the car is following a RECORDED PATH (a packed point stream in the
block DAT_000c0ce4). Enemy cars drive recorded lines and only use the car_AIComputeSteer AI when they are off a path.
Renamed: car_CrashStep -> car_PathStep 0x2a056, car_CrashEnd -> car_PathLeave 0x2a028, car_CrashSetSpin -> car_PathSetSpin
0x284f6, car_ForEachNearbyCar -> path_FindNearest 0x28366 (it searches paths, not cars), s_gotit_280ba -> path_NearestInRecord,
s_got_joy_ptr_0_27bff -> path_Advance 0x27bf5, fn_27b59 -> path_ReadPoint, fn_27b4c -> path_ReadWord, FUN_00027e81 ->
car_PathAttach (real entry 0x27e74, prologue outside the Ghidra function), FUN_00027f19 -> car_AIPathSeek, s_lev1_24188 ->
car_InitCommon, FUN_00023726 -> car_AnglesFromRecord, fn_3fb31 -> sys_Fatal (assert handler, ~70 callers).
- Path block DAT_000c0ce4: count, then offsets to records. Record: +0 short first track segment, +2 last segment (wraps at
  the segment count *DAT_000c0cdc>>16), +4 index-table size (dword>>17 entries), +6 offset to end, +10 index table of shorts
  (point-stream offset per segment; bit 0x8000 asserts), then the point stream. Point = header short: bit 0x8000 clear =
  absolute point (header<<16|short, then two dwords; 12 bytes), set = delta (14-bit dx in the header, dy, dz shorts; 6 bytes);
  bit 0x4000 = an extra dword follows (orientation record used by car_AnglesFromRecord). Coordinates are 12.4 (x16 = world).
- Per-car path state (wram): +0xf4 on path, +0xf8 path speed (16.16 points per tick), +0xfc position along the path (int =
  point index, frac = lerp), +0x100 record offset (0 = none), +0x104 current point offset, +0x108 index-table position,
  +0x10c direction +-1, +0x110..+0x118 current point, +0x11c..+0x124 next point, +0x12c orientation record, +0x174 start slot.
- path_FindNearest(world, skipRecord, callback, radius): nearest point of any other record within radius; result[0..8] =
  record, index, point offset, index pos, xyz, direction, distance; callback(world, &result). Callers: enecon01_Init
  (car_PathAttach, 0x5dc00), car_PathStep when a path ends and the car is not visible (car_PathAttach, 0x138800 = chain to the
  next path), car_DriveStepAI every tick off-path with countdown <= 0 (car_AIPathSeek, 0x1f400: attach if a point is within
  0x1900, else pull vel toward the point and the path direction).
- car_PathStep: path_Advance -> new pos; vel = new - old (so collisions still work); if visible, col_Trace old->new against the
  object list (type 9) and a hit -> car_PathLeave(0x14) = 20 ticks of plain forward push, then free AI. DAT_000c5bfc==1 =
  pre-start freeze (velocity zeroed).
- enecon01_Init: car_InitCommon, shared radius 64000 and mass 0x96 (150; player 500), attach to the nearest path within
  0x5dc00, start position from path_Advance, push state. DAT_000a6650 == 2 -> no enemy cars (world_QueueFree).
- enecon01_RateLoop: staggered start: slot n = wram+0x174 - 10; frozen until game tick n*15+10; between +10 and +50 the path
  speed is set to 100000 + (6-n)*2000 (16.16, ~1.5 points per tick; earlier slots faster) and car_EmitSpinParticle fires
  (launch wheelspin). DAT_000a6654==1 skips the stagger. Then car_PathStep or car_DriveStepAI; an enemy that is not visible
  with lap wram+0xe8 > 4 and progress > 0x32 is freed (world_QueueFree).
- car_InitCommon 0x24188 (bilstyring_Init, enecon01_Init, pickcon01_Init): track+0x4c = 1600000 (special table DAT_000ea600
  for the level named at DAT_000a1187), +0x74 = has-track flag from the first level-list entry, resets angles, shared+1 |= 1.

Next candidates: where DAT_000c0ce4 (paths) and DAT_000c0cdc (segment table) are loaded (grep the level loader for those

un-functioned 0x222f8 / 0x111b5 / 0x120300.

### Loaders: initglobal + path recorder (10-10, eighth pass)
hand_scordos.txt now 186 lines (xalign: 71 to SCORWIN, 16 to LORT). New script ghidra\scripts\DataRefs.java <addr>...
prints every reference to a global (`R addr TYPE from function | instruction`); filter with grep '^INFO  DataRefs.java> R'.

- initglobal_Init 0x2439f (world, routdat): routdat[1] -> recorded path block DAT_000c0ce4 (file_Load tag -3, then
  path_LoadFixup 0x24450), routdat[0] -> track segment table DAT_000c0cdc (file_Load + bswap16_at 0x40886 on both header
  halves); DAT_000c0ccc = *DAT_000c0cdc >> 16 = segment count. initglobal_CloseDown 0x24401 frees DAT_000c0ce4 (-3),
  DAT_000c0cd8 (0), DAT_000c0cdc (1) with mem_FreeRaw 0x40108 and clears DAT_000c0cf8.
- path_LoadFixup 0x24450 byte-swaps the whole path block in place (count, record offsets, record header shorts +4/+6/+8,
  index table shorts, every packed point incl. the 0x4000 orientation dword). The path files on disc are big-endian:
  authored on/for the Saturn build and swapped at load on PC.
- file_Load 0x4027d (&out, name, tag): file_Open 0x3ee31 (returns size, "file open error %s"), mem_AllocRaw 0x3ff1c
  ("total alloc %d %d"), file_ReadInto 0x3ef29 ("load buffer not alloced for %s", "file load error %s"). 118 call sites;
  the big level loaders are 0x30002 (33 calls) and 0x34c48 (30).
- Dev path recorder left in the binary: path_RecordFinish 0x29979 (no static caller; tags "PJOY" and "penis") builds one
  record from a per-segment table (DAT_000c0ccc entries, bit 0 = unresolved) and the recorded point buffer DAT_000c1d7c
  (16-byte points -> absolute 12-byte points + orientation extra), then path_InsertRecord 0x294fc ("JMOVS"/"NEWREC":
  rebuilds the block with the record replaced/appended) and FUN_00029126. path_DumpRecords 0x294b9 prints "rec nr %d size
  %d" via dbg_Printf 0x3f539. Record byte size = (rec+6>>16)+0xe.
- scboard_FrmupdateBody 0x2d8d3 = the real body of scboard_Frmupdate (Ghidra split): DAT_000c0cf4 = rec+0x30 (start
  progress), race position = car list walk on lap*DAT_000c0ccc+progress, HUD sprites via s_extram_18f02 + table
  DAT_000c0c18, race end -> DAT_000c0ce8 = 1 (autodrive) + placement sprite (switch on +0x60 = 1..6) -> s_title_2deba,
  countdown DAT_000c5c3c, fn_17875 level change ("title"/"hisetup"/"options"), snd_Play(0x128) on a new lap.
- track_SegDistance 0x1e383 (trk): segment list header at trk+0x74 -> +0x1c -> +4; DAT_000c0b98 = first 0x48-byte node
  (hdr + hdr[1] + 0x10), DAT_000c0b94 = end (hdr[0] nodes); trk+0x1c = sum of node +0x24 lengths up to the current node
  trk+0x38 + fixmuldiv(trk+0x40, seglen, trk+0x40 + trk+0x44); then fn_15511. s_SVIN_1e64e also writes b94/b98.
- Writers of the mode globals (DataRefs): DAT_000a6650 <- FUN_00031b6b, demosel_Display (2 = no enemies / title);
  DAT_000a6654 <- s_endseq_21cae (1 = no start stagger); DAT_000c0ce8 autodrive <- bilstyring_Init, scboard_FrmupdateBody,
  s_pisoglortelort_2d92d; DAT_000c0cec input off <- bilstyring_Init, scboard_FrmupdateBody, s_title_2deba;

### Level loaders and file formats: setup text, R0V pictures, bobs (10-10, tenth pass)
Decompile dump scratchpad ldr3.c (not kept). 224 hand names applied to scordos; xalign 465 groups, 1 conflicting, carried
91 to SCORWIN, 22 to LORT.
- CORRECTION: 0x30002 and 0x34c48 are NOT level loaders. 0x30002 = title_LoadPics (33 l:\titlegfx\*.R0V title/menu
  pictures into DAT_000c5edc..DAT_000c5fc8, no static caller, Ghidra split entry) and 0x34c48 = optionsmain_Init (30 more).
- R0V picture format, r0v_Convert 0x30455 (same code in LORT 0x060d339c): magic "PARGB15" (7 bytes), be32 width +8, be32
  height +0xc, then height be32 row offsets (-1 = empty row) into span data that follows the table (offset*2 bytes); a row =
  spans {u16 skip, u16 count, count x ARGB1555} until a 0 word. Header is rewritten in place to [w][h][rowtable ptr].
  Big-endian on disk again = Saturn-authored.
- The real level loader chain, from eng_StartLevel:
  level_LoadSetup 0x1721c: setups\<lev>\<lev>.txt -> ctx+0x144 (ctx = DAT_000bf80c), .cmp/.pal names in ctx+0xf8/+0xfc
  (pal_LoadLevel 0x202fa: level .cmp/.pal if file_Exists 0x3f021 else pal_LoadDefault 0x202ba c:\3dzwork\default.*;
  DAT_000bf9f0 = cmp, DAT_000bf9f4 = pal, entry cmp[0] forced black = transparent index). Tokens by first char:
  W -> level_LoadWorldEntry 0x16ecc ("W<name> <id> <setnr> <flag> [<extra if flag 0>]": worlds\<n>\<n>.wl3 +
       setups\<lev>\worlds\<n>\set<setnr>.bin -> level_LoadWorld 0x16d45: slot = world_SlotByName 0x16b6c in the ctx+0x40
       table of 0x14-byte slots (+8 instance, +0xc wl3 data); loads the .wl3 once (s_WARNING_got_prio_over_li_1f50f = chunk
       prepare, flag 0 -> s_BIG3_15544 bigs list), loads the set bin, instance = fn_1d785(wl3, slot, set), wl3_BindAndPreInit)
  P -> level_LoadPathEntry 0x16fe3 ("P<name> <n>": paths\<name>\<n>.bin -> path_LoadFile 0x16e75, a chunk file, fn_1d785(.,0,0))
  R -> level_LoadRoutEntry 0x17048 ("R<name> <setnr>": routs\<name>\<name>.bin + setups\<lev>\routs\<name>\set<setnr>.bin ->
       rout_LoadFile 0x16aca: wl3_SwapChunk both, wl3_BindRoutines(rout, set, 0, DAT_000bf810, 0, 0)); lev1.txt has no R line.
  Each loaded block is registered with atab_Register 0x111d8 (DAT_000a64a4) for teardown. Names built with sprintf 0x40bdb
  and the data root PTR_s__000a6488. atoi 0x1006d.
- bigs_LoadStream 0x1567f (slot <= 100, n): bigs\<WORLD>.<n> (world name = wl3 data +0x4c) into the 0x48-byte entry n of
  the table at slot+4, entry+0x38 = 1; bigs_LoadList 0x15a22: bigs\<name>.ld -> FUN_000159dc.
- spr_LoadBobs 0x1dad7 (raybobs bf.fmz / bf.fzp; insins_PreInit and 0x2c98a): B = u16 count + u16 entries; A = u8 flag, u16,
  u16 count, be32 offsets to 5-short records tagged FP (0x5046) or FN (0x4e46); big-endian.

## World instances + the big-endian file preparer -- hand-named (10-10, eleventh pass)

Names in `ghidra\hand_scordos.txt` (236 names; xalign SCORWIN 99, LORT 23). Decompiles: scratchpad `inst.c` (not kept).

- world_CreateInstance 0x1d785 (data, slot, set): the first 4 bytes compared with "PATH" (DAT_000a0d33): path files
  -> path_CreateInstance 0x1d6d2, else world_CreateFull 0x1d160. Callers level_LoadWorld, path_LoadFile.
- wl3_PrepareFile 0x1f50f (data) = the in-place byte swapper for every loaded chunk file (same magic "PATH" at
  DAT_000a0e30 -> path_PrepareFile 0x1f076). Also in SCORWIN 0x00410513 and LORT 0x060f9b48.
- .wl3 header (big-endian on disk): +0 object offset table offset (count at +0x10), +4 hierarchy offset (0x14-byte
  nodes: u16, u16, 4 dwords; node u16 +0 > 0 = child count, walked with the pointer stack DAT_000bf7f8 / limit
  DAT_000bf7f4+8 "outofstack"), +8 sub-block offset (wl3_SwapSubBlock 0x1f3f2: 4 dwords then variable records,
  helpers FUN_0001f31d / FUN_0001f38d not read), +0xc u16 table (be16 count + entries), +0x14 ?, +0x18 total object
  RAM (FBits size), +0x1c 3x3 matrix, +0x40 pos, +0x5c world chunk list (wl3_SwapChunk). Name string at +0x4c.
- Object record (at data + table + offset): +0..+0x44 dwords, +0x48 u16 prio (clamped to 0x1f with the WARNING),
  +0x4a u16, +0x4c, +0x50, +0x54 (set to -2 after load), +100 chunk list (ANIM 0x414e494d, MATX 0x4d415458 ...).
  Three short streams: geometry at obj+obj[0] (n1 = be16, n2 = be16 at +2+n1*6; n1*3 + n2*4 + 2 shorts = n1 vertices
  + n2 quads?), obj+obj[1] with obj[6] (+0x18) * 3 shorts, obj+obj[5] (+0x14) with obj[7] (+0x1c) shorts. Streams are
  contiguous and shared between objects (the swapper takes the min/max over all objects and swaps once).
- Path file (PATH magic, paths\<level>\<n>.bin): +4 count, +8 dword offsets to sub-tables (count + offsets) of
  records (3 dwords + rec[0] x 0x48-byte track nodes from +0x14 = the nodes of track_SegDistance), +0x14 offset of the
  routine chunk list. path_CreateInstance binds only that chunk list (no objects, no ComRam).
- Runtime layout (world_CreateFull): WorldRAMHeader 0xbc via mem_AllocLinked 0x1275e (4-byte link word before the
  block, registered in ctx+0x14c); everything else via world_AllocSubLinked 0x12701 (hdr+0xa4 list): OBJRAMPTRS
  (count*4), one FBits block (wl3+0x18 bytes, per-object cursor advanced by routdat+0x1c), ObjRAMHeader 0x18 per object
  (+0 wl3, +4 object RAM, +0xc flags, +0x10 ext, +0x14 routdat = wl3_ObjRoutDat 0x12fd8), ext header 0x6c FALLOC only
  when the object has an ANIM chunk (+0 anim buf n*6, +4 buf, +8 matrix from MATX, +0x68 anim count).
- ComRam (world_AllocComRam 0x1d0b4) = the shared record hdr+0x98: 0x2c bytes, +0x24 PathRam 0x84 ("PathReturn" type)
  = the trk record of track_SegDistance / track_ProjectPos, +0x28 ImpactRam 0x84 ("ImpactDat") = the contact record of
  car_GroundResponse. Allocator tags come in pairs (type, instance): WorldRAMHeader/WorldRam, OBJRAMHEADER/OBJRAM.
- memset = 0x40bb0 (Watcom, calls fn_4af80); be16_read 0x1f010.
Next candidates: FUN_0001f31d / FUN_0001f38d (sub-block record swappers), FUN_0001f4c3 (set%s.bin), FUN_00010757 and
wl3_BindAndPreInit internals (the per-object PreInit calls), FUN_00012099 / FUN_000126e6 (link list bookkeeping),
s_BIG3_15544 / fn_155fe (bigs streams), fn_15511 (called at the end of every loader), the geometry stream consumer
(renderer: who reads obj[0] / obj[1] / obj[5]).

## .wl3 sub-block tree swappers -- hand-named (10-10, twelfth pass)

239 names in `ghidra\hand_scordos.txt` (xalign SCORWIN 99, LORT 23). Decompile: scratchpad `sub.c`; disassembly via
capstone (`py -3.13 -I dis.py scordos.bin <addr>:<len>`), which was needed because the decompiles were mangled.

- The sub-block at wl3 + wl3[+8] (wl3_SwapSubBlock 0x1f3f2) is a tree, not a list: 4 dwords, then records from +0x10 reached
  only through pushed links on the pointer stack DAT_000bf7f8.
- Branch record (sub_SwapNode8 0x1f38d, first byte 1..0x7f): 8 dword slots. Slot 0 = unswapped type/count short + be16 child
  offset; slots 1..7 = be32 with the top byte flags and the low 24 bits a child offset (relative to the end of the slot).
  Nonzero offsets are pushed. 8 children per branch = octree-like spatial partition of the world (collision/culling).
- Leaf record (sub_SwapLeaf 0x1f31d, first byte 0 or bit 7 set): header word stays unswapped (byte 0 bits 0..6 = entry count,
  byte 1 = child mask); one be16 offset (in shorts) per set mask bit, high bit first, pushed; then the entries: be16 word, low
  byte bits 0..5 = n, bit 6 clear = n inline bytes padded to even, set = n shorts (swapped).
- fn_15511 was never a function: 0x15511 is the shared epilogue (pop ebp edi esi edx ecx ebx; ret), 0x15512 the no-ebp entry.
  Every "then fn_15511" in the earlier sections means "tail-jumps to the epilogue". Now epilog_15511 (like epilog_2e5b5).
Next candidates: the runtime reader of the +8 tree (col_Trace / culling: grep decompiles for `+ 8)` on the wl3 pointer),
FUN_0001f4c3 (set%s.bin), FUN_00010757 and wl3_BindAndPreInit internals, FUN_00012099 / FUN_000126e6, s_BIG3_15544 /
fn_155fe, the geometry stream consumer.

## The .wl3 octree at runtime: collision face collection -- hand-named (10-10, thirteenth pass)

242 names in `ghidra\hand_scordos.txt`. Decompiles: scratchpad `mesh.c`, `mesh2.c` (not kept). The twelfth-pass
"sub_SwapLeaf / sub_SwapNode8" are now octree_SwapNode16 0x1f31d / octree_SwapNode32 0x1f38d.

- The +8 sub-block IS an octree over world space: root = wl3 + wl3[8]: x, y, z, size (cube), nodes from +0x10.
  Node32 (first byte 1..0x7f): 8 child links (slot 0 u16 at +2, slots 1..7 dwords), child k = octant k with bit0 +x,
  bit1 +y, bit2 +z, child cube = half size. Node16 (first byte 0 or >= 0x80): bits 0..6 entry count, byte 1 child mask,
  u16 child offsets in shorts, then the entries. Entry u16 header: bits 15..7 object index, bit 6 16-bit face indices,
  bits 5..0 face count; payload = face indices (bytes padded to even, or shorts).
- Reader: col_SegVsMesh 0x120d24 -> octree_CollectFaces 0x1212e2 (walker; 64-entry table DAT_001217fc maps the 6
  box-vs-midplane overlap bits to an octant mask; octree_PushNode16Children 0x1214cb) -> col_MeshTestFaces 0x120e1a.
- FBits = face bits: ObjRAMHeader +4 points at the object's slice of the FBits block, one byte per face (routdat +0x1c =
  face count, wl3 +0x18 = total). Bit 1 = collected in the current query. ObjRAMHeader +8 = transient list of collected
  face records {faces..., facebits, prev}; touched objects are listed in DAT_00120028..DAT_0012002c.
- Object mesh record (obj = wl3 + table + offset): +0 index stream (u16 n1, n1 triangles of 3 u16, u16 n2, n2 quads of
  4 u16; faces 0..n1-1 are the triangles; vertex indices premultiplied by 2), +4 vertex stream (3 shorts each, count +0x18),
  +8 per-face 4-byte table (signed bytes cx, ?, cz, r = XZ bounding circle in object units), +0x14 per-face u16 flags
  (count +0x1c; 0x10 = quad is tested as two triangles), +0x28 scale (object units = world / (scale<<2)),
  +0x2c..+0x34 position, +0x48 prio.
- col_MeshTestFaces converts the segment to object space (DAT_00120200.. endpoints, DAT_00120040[8] radius), does the
  face circle test, copies the polygon into DAT_00120104 (+0xa flags, +0xc vertex count, verts from +0x138 down) and runs
  FUN_001212a1 (segment vs polygon, not read); hit -> col+0x38 t, FUN_001211b0 back to world: col+0x2c hit point,
  +0x20 normal. The far code object (0x120000+) uses function-pointer slots DAT_0012000c / 0x120018 / 0x12003c filled by
  s_STACK_21277 and tail-jumps through DAT_0012003c instead of returning.
Next candidates: FUN_001212a1 (segment vs polygon = the actual collision maths), FUN_00121049, FUN_001211b0, s_STACK_21277
(the far-object import table), the renderer (who else reads the index/vertex streams), FUN_0001f4c3 set file,
wl3_BindAndPreInit internals, bigs streams.

## The segment-vs-polygon test: swept sphere collision maths -- hand-named (10-10, fourteenth pass)

256 names in `ghidra\hand_scordos.txt` (xalign: SCORWIN 99, LORT 23). Decompiles: scratchpad poly.c / poly2.c / poly3.c
(far-object code is EBP-relative scratch; 0x1212a1 and 0x1218fc read with capstone). Scale: object-space fixed point, dot
products are 64-bit then >> 16; t is 16.16 along the segment (0x10000 = the far end).

### Call chain (all far code object, EBP = polygon record DAT_00120104)
col_MeshTestFaces 0x120e1a -> per face col_RecordFaceHit 0x1212a1 -> col_SweptSphereVsPoly 0x121a05:
1. col_PolySetup 0x123103: edge vectors +0x54 (v1-v2), +0x3c (v2-v3); dir +0xb4 = end +0x108 - start +0xfc;
   normal +0xd8 = normalize(cross) to length PTR_FUN_001200b8 via vec_InvLength 0x121956 (x87 sqrt).
2. col_SphereVsPlane 0x122964: d = (start - v3).n >> 16 (+0x168); dir.n (+0x16c) >= 0 -> miss (back-facing / leaving);
   0 <= d <= r -> type 4 touching, t = 0; d < -r -> miss; t = (d - r) / -(dir.n); t < 0 miss; t > best t (+0x20) -> 1
   (reject, nearer hit exists); contact +0x24 = start + dir*t; plane point +0x30 = contact - n*r;
   col_PointInPoly 0x122f4a (drops the dominant normal axis, edge cross-product sign test, 3 or 4 verts by +0xc).
   Hit: best t = t, +0x144 contact, +0x150 plane point, +0x15c normal, +1 = 1, +0 = type.
3. If no face hit and flags +0xa & 0xf (edge-test enable bits): col_PolyEdgeSetup 0x123239 (extra edge vectors, unit dir
   +0xc0, edge flags +2..+5 = 1), col_SphereVsEdge 0x1221d8 per edge (descriptor at EDI: mask, vertex off, unit edge off,
   edge vector off, flag slots; perpendicular components +0x1a0 / +0x1ac; quadratic in t; contact must project inside the
   edge) -> type 2; then col_SphereVsVertex 0x121b42 for every edge whose flag is still 0 (perp distance < r, quadratic
   |rel|^2 - r^2, |dir|^2, 2 rel.dir) -> type 3, contact = the vertex.
   col_SolveSweepQuadratic 0x1225ef (64-bit coefficients c, b, a at EDI; c <= 0 -> t = 0 contact = start; smallest root in
   0..best t -> +0x1c, +6 solved) uses col_EvalQuadratic64 0x1218fd (Horner, real entry 0x1218fc).
4. col_RecordFaceHit on hit flag +1: DAT_00120044 = 1, col = DAT_00120040[7]: +0 = polyrec+7 kind, +1 = contact type
   (1 face, 2 edge, 3 vertex, 4 touching, 5 static), +2 u16 face index, facebits[face] |= 4, +0xc = DAT_00120034 (hit
   ObjRAMHeader; 0 for cars). Afterwards col_MeshTestFaces copies best t -> col+0x38, col_LocalToWorld 0x1211b0 (scale =
   routdat+0x28 << 2, + routdat+0x2c.., then DAT_00120014 world matrix) -> hit point col+0x2c and normal col+0x20.

### Polygon record (DAT_00120104, EBP-relative)
+0 contact type, +1 hit flag, +2..+5 edge flags, +6 solved, +7 kind, +0xa face flags (low nibble = edge tests on),
+0xc vertex count (3/4), +0x10 radius, +0x1c root t, +0x20 best t, +0x24 contact, +0x30 plane/contact point, +0x3c/+0x54
edge vectors, +0x48/+0x60/+0x6c extra edges, +0xb4 dir, +0xc0 unit dir, +0xd8 normal, +0xfc start, +0x108 end,
+0x114 v3 (quads), +0x120 v0, +0x12c v1, +0x138 v2, +0x144 saved contact, +0x150 saved point, +0x15c saved normal,
+0x168 d, +0x16c dir.n, +0x188/+0x1a0/+0x1ac/+0x1b8 edge-test scratch, +0x1c4/+0x1d0 vertex-test scratch.
col_SegMidCircle 0x121049: coarse circle (midpoint >> 8, (half length + radius) >> 8 at EDI+0x64..+0x70) for the per-face
byte circle test. FUN_001214a1 = the loop body of octree_CollectFaces (Ghidra split), named octree_CollectFaces_body.

Next candidates: s_STACK_21277 (far-object import table: what DAT_00120014/0x120018 are), the renderer (index/vertex
stream readers), FUN_0001f4c3 set file, wl3_BindAndPreInit internals, bigs streams, car_CollideStep response using
col+1 contact type, FUN_00121d9d / 0x121ebe / 0x121f4e (unread far-object functions).

## The far-object import table and engine boot -- hand-named (10-10, fifteenth pass)

284 names in `ghidra\hand_scordos.txt` (xalign: 104 SCORWIN, 24 LORT). Decompiles: scratchpad imp.c / imp2.c / imp3.c.

### eng_Boot 0x21277 (was s_STACK_21277; Ghidra split, the tail 0x2138c is eng_Boot_body = old "eng_Init" 0x21389)
1. data root: arg EDX or default PTR_DAT_000a6484 -> PTR_s__000a6488 (every loader sprintf prefix); DAT_000bf994 = 5.
2. sys_PlatformInit 0x3d8c9 (DOS: returns 1; abort on 0). mem_AllocRaw(&DAT_000bf7f4, 0x2000, "STACK"), DAT_000bf7f8 = top.
3. far_SetSlot 0x120608 (`mov [eax],edx; ret`) fills the import slots of the two hand-written far code objects:
   | slot | function | what the far code gets |
   |---|---|---|
   | 0x120004 | far_FatalMCExit 0x10099 | sys_FatalMsg("MC EXIT", string 0xa0029) |
   | 0x120008 | far_DbgPrintf 0x100aa | dbg_Printf |
   | 0x12000c | vec_WorldToLocalPos 0x106c1 | p -= world pos +0x20, vec_ToLocal |
   | 0x120010 | col_WorldToLocalAtTime 0x106e2 | p -= (pos - (1-t) * shared vel (+0x98)+0x18), vec_ToLocal; not a Ghidra function |
   | 0x120014 | vec_LocalToWorldPos 0x1075d | inverse |
   | 0x120018 | vec_ToWorld 0x105bf | rotate only |
   | 0x12001c | x87_Store3Ints 0x1064d | pops 3 x87 results into ints (ROUND) |
   | 0x155d90 | far_Fatal 0x17441 | renderer object fatal (reader s_FATAL_ERROR_tried_to_dra_1568b6) |
4. renderer object init func_0x156764, func_0x15e387 (second far object 0x150000+, draw tables 0x15d190/0x15d517/0x15d890/0x15db50/0x15de20/0x15e0d0).
5. gfx_InitGouraudMulTable 0x154b1: GRDMUL 0x400 bytes at DAT_000c0a70, [i][j] = (i*j)>>5, 32x32.
6. spr_InitBlitTable 0x1578a: blitter table DAT_000c0a74 (mode, data table ptrs, blit fns spr_BlitScaledTint16 0x1350e / 0x136c7 / 0x13b02 / 0x13cde).
7. eng_PushContext 0x12f3d: 0x154-byte "SEQPTR" context, +0x34 = previous, DAT_000bf80c = current. eng_PopContext 0x12f7c restores. The level loop pushes/pops the same way; eng_FreeLists 0x11153 (n=0x14, lists at ctx+0x2c, 0x14 bytes per head) at level end and boot.
8. eng_Boot_body: eng_InitTables 0x110cb (0x14, &DAT_000bf9ac), atab_Register(DAT_000a64a0), rout_install registration (s_install_3a2f1), rout_CallSystem(0) = PreInit, eng_FreeLists, eng_PopContext, symbol table s_SYMLNTAB_402f84, fn_17875(level name or "ilogos") = first level change, DAT_000bf994 = 4.

### Renderer face pass (far object, first reader of the +8 circle stream)
rend_CullFaces 0x12069c (hasFaces, ?, vertsPerFace, rec): rec[0] vertex seen table, [1] per-face normal bytes (3 signed), [2] per-face circle bytes (cx ? cz r), [3] visible-face list (shorts, grows down), [4] transform cache counter (-6 per new vertex), [5] vertex index list, [6] camera record, [7] index stream, [8] face count, [9] face base, [10] verts per face, [0x13] flags bit 2 -> rend_CullFacesFrustum 0x12080f (radius byte*0x20; depth>>8 < cam+0x48 + r; lateral vs fov slopes cam+0x3c/+0x40 * cam+0x44) else rend_CullFacesBackface 0x12074d (normal . (circle centre - eye) >= 0). Camera record: +0xc..+0x14 eye in object space, +0x18.. depth axis, +0x24.. / +0x30.. lateral axes, +0x3c/+0x40 fov slopes, +0x44 scale, +0x48 far. Result count in DAT_00120044 (= bytes / 2*(n+1)). DAT_00120024 reused as scratch.
gfx_ExpandRamp64 0x12060b: 64-wide 8-bit image (clamp 0x20) through the 33-entry u16 ramp DAT_00120654. spr_BlitScaledTint16 0x1350e: rec [0] dest [1] src [2] 16-bit pal [3] w [4] h [5] -> tint RGB555 [9]/[10] dest w/h [0xb] flip flags; pal 0xffff transparent; FUN_000132a1 per pixel.

### Collision helpers filled in
col_SetSegment 0x120c1d (?, col, x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1, r) -> DAT_001201e8.. (polyrec +0xe4..+0xf8), DAT_0012011c (+0x18) then col_SegmentSetup 0x121d9d (dir +0xb4, unit dir +0xcc, +0x1dc = half length + r, +0x1e0 midpoint). col_ResetBestT 0x120d14 (DAT_00120124 = 0x10000). col_CapsuleVsSphere 0x121ebe (|mid - centre(+0x138)|^2 <= (+0x1dc + +0x14)^2) behind col_SegVsSphere; col_SweptSphereVsSphere 0x121f4e (perp distance < r1+r2, col_SolveSweepQuadratic -> +0x20) behind col_SegVsSphereHit (t -> col+0x38). col_SegVsMesh_exit 0x120e2a returns DAT_00120044; col_SphereVsPlane_touch 0x122eea = split touching branch.

Gotchas: 0x21389 first 3 bytes are not code (real continuation 0x2138c); col_PointInPoly 0x122f4a / col_EvalQuadratic64 0x1218fd are listed one byte after their real entries; dis.py lengths are decimal.
