## The renderer frame pipeline -- hand-named (10-10, sixteenth pass)

54 names (hand_scordos.txt now 338 lines; xalign carried 116 to SCORWIN, 27 to LORT). Decompiles: scratchpad rend.c..rend5.c (not kept).
Everything below is the near-side (Watcom C) half of eng_RenderFrame 0x17d99; the far renderer object 0x150000+ only gets its entry far_Draw.

### Frame work block (DAT_000bf864, 0x70 bytes, allocated and freed in eng_RenderFrame)
[0] object count, [1] total faces (+= ROM obj +0x1c), [2] total vertices (+= ROM +0x18), [3]/+0xc draw list (objects x 0x24: world, ?, ObjRAMHeader,
face list, vertex list, vertex count, sort cursor, projected vertex base, object entry), [4]/[5] list ends (lists grow down), [6]/+0x18 polygon records
(0x2c each), [7]/+0x1c polygon cursor, [8]/+0x20 per-world view records (DAT_000bf804 x 0x60), [9]/+0x24 object entries (DAT_000bf800 x 0x14: world,
hdr, camera-space centre xyz), [0xa]/+0x28 sort list, [0xb]/+0x2c sort cursor (grows down), [0xc]/+0x30 sort end, [0xd]/+0x34 projected vertices
([0x1a] x 6 bytes: x,y,z shorts), [0x12]/+0x48 vertex seen table (DAT_000bf7f0 x 4, DAT_000bf7f0 = max vertices of one object), [0x13]/+0x4c vertex
index buffer, [0x14]/+0x50 face buffer, [0x18]/+0x60 sprite sort-list start, [0x19]/+0x64 visible faces, [0x1a]/+0x68 visible vertices.
DAT_000bf7ec = drawn object count; DAT_000bf868 = 0x4000-byte depth buckets; DAT_000bf7f8 = pointer stack (grows down) used by the tree walk and the
group lists. ACALC1 / ACALC21..24 / ACALC3 (DAT_000a0a7d..) are the mem tags of these lists (rend_Alloc 0x18bed / rend_Free 0x18e7f).

### Camera record PTR_DAT_000a64b8
+4..+0xc pos, +0x1c/+0x20/+0x24 projection terms (cam_ApplyProjection 0x1c283 from DAT_000a64a8..DAT_000a64b4 / DAT_000bf814 -> +0x30..+0x40),
+0x2c, +0x4c matrix (9 ints), +0x78/+0x7c near/far (used >>3), +0x80/+0x84/+0x88 fov terms, +0x8c, +0xf4 render mode (0 textured, 1 coloured,
2 flat), +0xf8 saved matrix. cam_BuildFrameCamera 0x1c593: pos = lerp of camera world DAT_000c0b80 +0x20 vs old +0x2c by (+0x18 - ctx+0x130)/33,
matrix = mat_Lerp 0x22718 (old +0x38, new +0x5c). mat_Lerp goes through 16-bit angles + mat_BuildRotation (DAT_000bfa04..).

### eng_RenderFrame order
1. cam_BuildFrameCamera; rend_AllocViewRecords 0x18c0f (block [8] [9]).
2. rend_BuildViewRecords 0x1becc: per world in ctx+0x20 list without flag bit0, world+0x94 = view record from rend_BuildViewRecord 0x1bc8a
   (0x60 bytes: [0..8] frustum axes in object space, [9..0xb] cam pos in object space, [0xc..0xe] lerped world pos, +0x3c world matrix).
3. rend_CollectObjects 0x1c232 -> rend_CollectWorldObjects 0x1c07c: walks the sphere tree in the world+8 chunk (+4 -> 0x14-byte nodes: +4 radius,
   +8 centre) with rend_SphereInFrustum 0x1bf11 (1 inside, -1 partial, 0 out); leaf word <= 0 = object -index (wl3_ObjRoutDat); ROM+0x54 progress
   (-2 = always) must be within camera progress ctx+0x108 +-9600000 wrapping at (ctx+0x44)+4; partial sets ConBits bit1 (needs clipping).
4. rend_TransformCentres 0x1b77e (object centres -> camera space); rend_AllocDrawLists 0x18c64 / body 0x18c85; world_CallSlotAll(5) Display.
5. rend_CullObjects 0x1b634 (was s_nrzeroobjs_d): per object with hdr+0xc bit2 clear, rend_BuildCullRecord 0x1b352 (+0x3c..+0x48 fov terms >>8,
   eye in object space, matrix from the view record or RAMext+8 when ROM+0x20 bit 0x40) then rend_CullObject 0x1b549 = two rend_CullFaces passes
   (quads, triangles; hdr+0xc bit 0x80 = no backface cull, bit 0x20 = vertices from RAMext+4).
6. rend_LatchSpriteList 0x18a96; rend_AllocPolyLists 0x18d37 (block [0xd] [0xa] [6]).
7. rend_ProjectObjects 0x1b879: per drawn object far_ProjectVertices 0x123637 (498 bytes, not a Ghidra function; args entry, verts, matrix, cursor,
   cam+0x2c, DAT_000bf85c, DAT_000bf860, cam+0x24); object matrix RAMext+8 (mat_Lerp with +0x38 when hdr+0xd bit2) or world+0x80 scale shorts.
8. rend_CheckSortValsAll 0x1602f (debug) when cam+0xf4 == 0; track_UpdateSegmentVisibility 0x17675 (ctx+0x140 ranges vs ctx+0x108, margins
   6400000 behind / 640000 ahead -> segment visible flags at ((ctx+0x40 + slot x 0x14)+4)+0x38 + seg x 0x48).
9. rend_BuildPolyList 0x1a4d9: 0x2c-byte polygon records: +0 group (obj+0x46 >> 16, 0..31), +2 type (0 colour, 1 flat, 2 textured; |4 when face
   flag 0x20; byte +3 |2 when 0x80), +4 colour (mode 1/2: pal[(flags >> 9) & 0x7f], col_RGB15ToIndex 0x13283 when 8 bpp), +8 bucket chain,
   +0xc material ptr (mode 0), +0x10 min z, +0x12 max z, +0x14.. z per corner, +0x1c.. screen xy per corner. Callbacks rend_PolyTextured 0x1a3e8 /
   rend_PolyColoured 0x1a425 / rend_PolyFlat 0x1a48f. Material table: set+0x14 -> [obj+0x4c] x 0x10 +4, 0x28 per face. Polygon kept if any corner
   0 < x < DAT_000bf854, 0 < y < DAT_000bf858 and no corner is 0x7fff. rend_EnvMapFaces 0x19c88 (ROM+0x20 bit 0x80 and ROM+0x10) = reflection
   mapping: eye vector reflected around the vertex normal in x87 doubles -> angles (fn_101ae atan) -> u,v into the material entry (chrome cars).
10. rend_AddSprites 0x19747: sprite list DAT_000c0b88 (ctx+0x124; +8 next, +0xc prev): +0x1a bit1 spr_AdvanceFrame 0x1905d, callback +0x10, image
    DAT_000c0c18[+0x14] or spr_MaterialImage 0x18b5c, +0x1a bit0 spr_Build2D 0x190b0 else spr_Move 0x19272 + spr_Project3D 0x192fe (was s_here;
    spr_ProjectPoint 0x181a3; billboard when +0x19 bit 8, image <= 200 px else sys_Fatal; else 4 projected corners, depth < 0x200); record type 10;
    life word 0 -= DAT_000c0b84, <= 0 -> spr_Remove 0x18abd.
11. rend_SortPolys 0x1ae71: rend_BucketPolys 0x1acab (bucket = ((minz + maxz) >> 17) & 0x7ff, chained via +8, counts per group), rend_AllocGroupLists
    0x1ae18 (from DAT_000bf7f8), rend_GatherBuckets 0x1ad12 (0x1000 buckets front to back), rend_MergeSorted 0x1ad66 -> block[0xb]. So the painter
    order is depth-bucketed then grouped (group = obj+0x46 >> 16, a texture page / draw group of the set).
12. rend_Draw 0x17d34: ctx+0x50 = 8 or 0x10 bpp, +0x5c frame block, +0x60 work buffer DAT_000bf84c, +0x7c DAT_000bf850 -> far_Draw 0x157890 (ctx)
    then rend_DrawDone 0x17d78 -> rend_DrawHighColour 0x1106a (empty: 24/32 bpp never implemented). far_Draw copies ctx[1..10] into
    DAT_00157860.., polygon list = block +0x2c..+0x30 (DAT_00155e2c, count DAT_00155e74), PTR_FUN_00157947 setup, init table PTR_PTR_0015cdbb, then
    FUN_0015794b = the draw loop (next pass).
13. rend_FreeLists 0x18e12; dbg_TextAt 0x3f6d7 (debug rows DAT_000c7fd4, 200 chars each); free block.
Also: far_SetScreenSize 0x123332 / rend_SetScreenSize 0x1567b6 (from eng_SetVideoMode), fx_RotoZoomRow8/16 0x123829/0x123a49 (dead rotozoom
fillers), vec_RotateStore3 0x105cc, stub_Nop_13afa.

### Gotchas
- A quoted heredoc of ~50 long lines fails in the Bash tool with "unexpected EOF while looking for matching" even without quotes in the text;
  shorter heredocs work. Fall back to the Write tool for big name files.
- s_ACALC1_18c24 is the IMPORTED name but the Ghidra function starts at 0x18c0f; s_ACALC22_18c85 is the body of the 31-byte wrapper 0x18c64.

Next candidates: FUN_0015794b far draw loop + PTR_FUN_00157947 / PTR_PTR_0015cdbb tables (the span fillers of the far renderer 0x150000+),
far_ProjectVertices 0x123637 (make it a function), FUN_0001607e / s_UFLP_14545, FUN_00019afd / FUN_0001993d (env-map rotation), FUN_0001f4c3 set
file, wl3_BindAndPreInit internals, bigs streams, car_CollideStep response, LORT Saturn-only code (SCU DSP use? see video notes 10-10).

## The far renderer draw loop -- hand-named (10-10, seventeenth pass)

63 names (401 total). far_Draw 0x157890 -> far_PatchScreenBounds 0x157bf8 (writes DAT_00155ddc/de0/de4/de8 into the compare immediates 0x15799b..0x157ab6; the decompiler shows 0x12345677/8) -> far_DrawPolys 0x15794b (loop over the sorted pointer list DAT_00155e2c, count DAT_00155e74).

Per 0x2c polygon record: flag 0x80 -> far_DrawSprite 0x1568b6 (called at 0x1568b5). Corners +0x1c..+0x2a -> 16.16 x on the stack (x*0x10000+0x8000) with outcodes 4/2/1 and y with 0x20/0x10/8; DAT_00157854 = stack offset of the topmost corner (0x18/0x10/8/0); AND of outcodes & 0x2d -> rejected; sum 0x12 = all inside; else clip tables (+0x50) and x clamped +-0xfa00000, y +-4000. Table row = (bpp-8)>>1 + variant (flags & 0x12c: 0 -> +0, 4 -> +8, 0x100 -> +0x10, 8 -> +0x18, 0x20 -> +0x20) + 0xa0 when flag 0x200. Rows are 0x28 bytes (5 variants x 8bpp/16bpp pair); the +0x28/+0x78/+0xc8/+0x118 rows are never selected (their targets are not functions).

- flag 1 = LINE polygons (wireframe), table PTR_FUN_0015cdd0: far_LinePoly8 0x158374 / far_LinePoly16 0x158694, variant flag 4 = colour key (index 0 draws nothing) far_LinePoly8Key 0x1589d4 / far_LinePoly16Key 0x158d04; lines via far_Line8 0x158414 (entry 0x15840f), far_Line16 0x15874f, far_Line8Key 0x158a7c (entry 0x158a77), far_Line16Key 0x158dbf: Cohen-Sutherland against DAT_00155d94/da8/d9c/dac then Bresenham, colour DAT_00155e6c.
- flag 2 = textured, table PTR_FUN_0015d050, called with (texture index = byte mat[1]+0xe, mat[1]+4, mat+8..+0x24): far_TexPoly8 0x1599f0, far_TexPoly16 0x15a310 (variants _Key 4 / _F100 / _F8 / _F20; 8bpp shares one routine for 4 and 0x100), clipped far_TexPoly8Clip 0x159eb0 / far_TexPoly16Clip 0x15ab60, flag 0x200 rows far_TexPoly8B 0x15b360 / far_TexPoly16B 0x15bcf0 / far_TexPoly8BClip 0x15b840 / far_TexPoly16BClip 0x15c590. Texture = DAT_00135b84 + index*0x200.
- colour table 0x15cf10 (targets 0x1590c4 / 0x159294, clipped 0x159494 / 0x159734): not Ghidra functions yet, not read.

Edge walkers (init table PTR_PTR_0015cdbb = {0x157c85, 0x157edb}, Ghidra entries 9 bytes later): far_EdgeWalk 0x157c8e (no clip) and far_EdgeWalkClip 0x157ee4. Both: DAT_00157850 edges left, DAT_00155dec/df0 left/right corner offsets, DAT_00155df4/df8 rows left per edge, edge steps = (delta*4) * DAT_00125b80[dy] (reciprocal table, 2^30/n) for x and two attributes (u at stack+0, v at +4), patched into their own code; per scanline the span routine (its address patched at uRam00157e4f/51 by each filler) gets (row ptr DAT_00155dfc, du, dv). far_SetRowStride 0x157ebe patches the row stride. far_NextPoly 0x157c76 = loop re-entry after a filler.

Depth fog: max z rec+0x12 >= 0x200 -> level (z-0x200)>>5 capped 15; 8bpp table _DAT_0015786c + level*0x100 (16 x 256 remap), 16bpp DAT_0015787c + level*0x40 and _DAT_00157880 + level*0x800. Span routines: far_TexSpan8 0x159a40 / far_TexSpan8Fog 0x159c9f / far_TexSpan16 0x15a360 / far_TexSpan16Fog 0x15a730 / far_TexSpan8Clip 0x159ef7 / far_TexSpan8ClipFog 0x15a136.

Sprites (far_DrawSprite): image rec+0x20 with tag 18000 (0x4650 "FP", the bob tag of spr_LoadBobs) else "FATAL ERROR tried to draw unknown"; [0] w, [1] h, +0xc.. ushort row offsets (0 = empty), rows = RLE runs {skip, count (0x7f = ushort), texels} to a 0 byte; colour map rec+0x28+2; scaled into rec+0x1c..+0x26 with steps (w<<18)*recip(dx); 8 inlined variants: bpp x flag 0x20 (texel 0 transparent) x x-clipped.

Time-sliced blitters (no direct callers, reached by patched jumps; pixel budget DAT_00156684, suspended flag DAT_00155e80, saved state DAT_00156688[12]): blit_Lit8 0x15d3eb / blit_Lit16 0x15d6e9 (15-bit texel x three interpolated 5-bit light channels through the 32x32 product table ctx+0x28), blit_SlicedLight8 0x15d993 / blit_SlicedLight16 0x15df1a (4-bit light map, gamma table DAT_00155e84), blit_SlicedTex8 0x15dc05 / blit_SlicedTex16 0x15e17c (guess). Who sets them up is unknown (candidates: the title picture / r0v or environment code).

Gotchas: all far fillers tail-jump into far_NextPoly, so Ghidra inlines the whole dispatcher loop into each of them (read only the head before "DAT_00155e74 = DAT_00155e74 + -1"). xalign matched only far_DrawSprite in SCORWIN (0x4b18b5); the far code is not call-linked, so SCORWIN/LORT far names need their own pass.

Next candidates: the colour fillers 0x1590c4 / 0x159494 (create functions first), who calls the blit_* routines (search for writes to DAT_00156684), far_ProjectVertices 0x123637, FUN_0001607e / s_UFLP_14545, FUN_00019afd / FUN_0001993d, FUN_0001f4c3 set file, wl3_BindAndPreInit internals, bigs streams, car_CollideStep response, LORT Saturn-only code / SCU DSP use, SCORWIN far renderer (0x4b18b5 region).

## Texture depack and colour fillers -- hand-named (10-10, eighteenth pass)

29 new + 8 renamed (430 hand names). Pass 17's blit_Lit*/blit_Sliced* were misnamed: they are the inner loops of the texture depack blitters, not frame-time fillers.

Colour fillers (flat colour polygons, colour table 0x15cf10 = line table 0x15cdd0 + 0x140; row = (bpp-8)>>1 + variant + 0x50 clipped): far_ColPoly8 0x1590c4 (colour rec+4 & 0xff, pitch patched into 0x159261), far_ColPoly16 0x159294 (colour & 0x7fff, fog = *(u16*)(_DAT_00157880 + level*0x800 + (c&0x3ff)*2) | ECX table[(c>>10)*2], pitch into 0x15944f), far_ColPoly8Clip 0x159494 (colour in DAT_00155e70, pitch into 0x159704, clip y DAT_00155de4/de8, x DAT_00155ddc/de0), far_ColPoly16Clip 0x159734. All fall into far_NextPoly. Textured table 0x15d050 still has unnamed entries: 159c50 15a6d0 159e10 15a910 15aa40 159d70 15a840 159ce0 15a780 15a0f0 15aef0 15a280 15b110 15b240 15a1f0 15b050 15a170 15afa0 (+ the 0xa0 rows 15b3f0..15cca0).

Texture depack (bigs streams -> per-object composed textures, time-sliced): tex_DepackObject 0x1444e (worldSlot, bigsIndex, objIndex; entry = slot[1] + bigs*0x48; header [0] object count, +0x28 object table of 0xc: [0] desc count, +8 self-rel offset to 8-byte descriptors; "depack object fuckup" fatal) -> tex_DepackLoop 0x143dd (ctx+0x88..+0xa0; DAT_000c0b04 = suspended; done: ctx+0xb4 = 0, (entry+0x14)[obj] = 1) -> tex_DepackOne 0x14a83. Descriptor 8 bytes: u16 A image, u16 B image (-1 none), byte +6 flags (1 flip x, 2 flip y, 4 transpose), byte +7 bit 1 exact UV range, bit 0 -> cache [2]. Image rec 8 bytes (ctx+0x94 A / +0x98 B): byte w, byte h, u16 palette index (ctx+0x90 self-rel table), dword +4 pixel offset. Cache rec 0x14 at entry+0x18 + idx*0x14: [0] use count, [1] pixels, [2] flag, [3] u16 size, +0xe w, +0xf h. UV rec 0x28 at ctx+0x84 (advances): [1] cache rec, [2..9] corners 16.16. Blit record 15 dwords: [0] dest [1] A px [2] A pal [3] wA [4] hA [5] B px [6] B pal [7] wB [8] hB [9] dest w [10] dest h [0xb] flags [0xc] GRDMUL DAT_000c0a70 [0xd] cmp DAT_000bf9f0 [0xe] ctx+0x80 (budget = [0xe]+0x48 = ctx+0xc8). Pick: B 2x2 -> tex_DepackGouraud 0x16794 (10x10 spread buffer DAT_000c0b08), B present -> tex_DepackMul 0x168aa (dest = max(A,B) per axis), B 1x1 -> tex_DepackTint 0x16972 (cap 100x100), else 1x1 solid. tex_DepackContinue 0x15313 (ctx, budget) = the per-frame tick, returns 1 when the budget is spent. tex_AllocPixels 0x15518 = mem_Alloc(PTR_DAT_000a64fc, size, -3). Converters col_CmpLookup 0x132a1 (15-bit -> 8-bit via the 32K .cmp DAT_000bf9f0), col_RGB15Pass 0x132c3 (identity, 16bpp); swap_Int2 0x134f7.

Blitter table DAT_000c0a74 (tex_InitBlitTable 0x1578a): 4 entries of 0x24 indexed by bpp/8-1; +0 bytes/pixel, +4 blit_Gouraud8/16 0x15d190/0x15d517 (B = 4 RGB555 corners, 3 channels 16.16 interpolated, 32x32 product table rec[0xc]; loops 0x15d3eb/0x15d6e9), +0xc blit_Mul8/16 0x15d890/0x15de20 (A texel x B 4-bit map colour, lanes x*0x8001 & 0x1f07c1f, 1024-entry product table DAT_00155e84; loops 0x15d993/0x15df1a), +0x10 blit_Mul8T/16T 0x15db50/0x15e0d0 (transposed; loops 0x15dc05/0x15e17c), +0x14 tex_ScaledTint8/16 0x1350e/0x13b02, +0x18 tex_ScaledTint8T/16T 0x136c7/0x13cde. Suspend: DAT_00155e80 flag, DAT_00156688 saved state (0x29 dwords gouraud, 12 mul); A palette 0xffff = transparent -> 0.

Sprite frames (KFRM): bob_LoadFrames 0x1e197 (&out, data, pal): u16 format (0 RLE, 1 packed 4-bit), u16 count, count u32 frame offsets (u16 w|h, 16-entry RGB555 palette at +2, pixels); 0x3c records = 0x28 UV rec + 0x14 image rec; decoders bob_DecodeRle16 0x1dd25, bob_Decode4To16 0x1dc46, bob_DecodeRle8 0x1df21, bob_Decode4To8 0x1de14. RLE row: u16 offset (0 = empty), then [skip][run (0x7f = 0x7f + next)][indices]... until 0; transparent 0xffff / 0. FUN_0001e027(data, count) runs first (unexplored).

Gotchas: the blit_* loops are entered by fall-through from the wrappers (not real functions); dis.py must start on an instruction boundary; big heredocs for the name files fail in Bash (use Write).

Next candidates: FUN_0001e027 (KFRM pre-pass), the unnamed textured-table fillers 0x159c50.. (compare with far_ColPoly8 to find the texture/UV variants), far_ProjectVertices 0x123637, FUN_0001607e / s_UFLP_14545, FUN_0001f4c3 (set file), callers of tex_DepackObject / tex_DepackContinue (which frame tick calls the depacker), LORT Saturn-only code (SH-2 VDP1 side).

## Texture streaming -- hand-named (10-10, nineteenth pass)

33 new + 1 renamed (463 hand names). The per-frame texture streamer that feeds the depacker of pass 18.

Entry: tex_StreamTick 0x1607e (from eng_RenderFrame; Ghidra split continuation 0x160a1): tex_DepackTick 0x15305 (real entry; 0x15313 was tex_DepackContinue, now tex_DepackTick_body; budget DAT_000a650c rows) -> busy return; while free texture-heap bytes DAT_000bfa28 < 200000: tex_EvictOne 0x1521a; tex_RequestByTrack 0x16108; rend_CheckSortValsAll; tex_UpdateQueue 0x15f55; tex_LoadUrgentQueue 0x14603 (bucket 16, all, unlimited budget) else the first request of buckets 15..0 -> tex_LoadObject (time-sliced).

Request queue at ctx+0x118: 34 buckets of 8 bytes ([0] head, [1] tail); 0..16 pending by priority, 17..33 loaded (priority + 0x11). Nodes from the pool ctx+0x110..+0x114 (pointer array, capacity DAT_000a6508; tex_AllocNode 0x15d5d "Alloc DataTab too small", tex_FreeNode 0x15ce9): 8 dwords [0] bucket (-1 none) [1] wanted prio [2] slot [3] bigs [4] obj [5] next [6] prev [7] pool slot. tex_LinkNode 0x15ef6 (head push; dbg when prio > 0x21), tex_UnlinkNode 0x15ea2. Per object: p = (bigs entry[5] + obj*0x10): [0] ready, [1] UV record list, [2] request node. tex_Request 0x15db4 (slot, bigs, prio 0..15, obj; "Alloc SortVal out of range"): new node or raise prio; called by rend_CheckSortVals (visibility) and tex_RequestByTrack (track instance list ctx+0x44: [0] slot, [1] ring length, [2] ring head, [3] object refs x5 dwords ([0] track pos, [1] obj, [2] bigs), [4] count, [5] next; camera track pos ctx+0x108; prio = max((0x800000-d)>>1 ahead, 0x400000-(len-d) behind) then mulhi32(prio, 0x3400) 0x120305). Eviction cursor ctx+0x11c walks loaded buckets from the tail of 17.

Loading: tex_LoadObject 0x14545 (slot, bigs, obj; was s_UFLP; allocs the UV list desc count*0x28 tag UFLP, tex_AllocCacheTable 0x1541a (was s_BIG2: header[0x24] images x 0x14, tag BIG2) on first use, entry+0x10 = use count) -> tex_DepackObject. Synchronous: tex_LoadObjectNow 0x16261 (prio 0x21), tex_ReloadAll 0x15135 (eng_SetVideoMode: tex_FreeAll 0x1508d then everything, then tex_PreloadByTrack), tex_PreloadByTrack 0x16341 (eng_StartLevel: progressive refinement along the ring, step = tex_MaxRingLength 0x16205, tex_RingStep 0x16243, step *= _DAT_000a0810 until < 1, stops when DAT_000bfa28 < 0x7d000). tex_FinishDepack 0x152e8, tex_ClearQueue 0x15048. Freeing: tex_FreeObject 0x14fa7 -> tex_FreePixels 0x158ae (cache rec use count-- per descriptor, tex_FreeBuffer 0x12f2c -> heap_Free 0x3a874). heap_Free: block header at block-0x20 ([-0x18] heap, [-0x14] size), DAT_000bfa28 += size, free list heap+4/+8/+0xc (heap_FindFreeSlot 0x3a64f "tried to free the same twice", heap_InsertFree 0x3a75f). sys_MCillegal 0x10055. bob_FreeFrames 0x1e027 (KFRM teardown), bob_FreeSet 0x1e093 (guess). FUN_00028c8a's "ref" to 0x15313 was an immediate (false xref).

Next candidates: the unnamed textured fillers 0x159c50 .. 0x15b240 in the textured table 0x15d050 (+ rows 15b3f0..15cca0), rend_CheckSortVals (how visibility turns into tex_Request priorities), callers of tex_FreeObject FUN_000150ad / FUN_0001773c / fn_17b1c, far_ProjectVertices 0x123637, FUN_0001f4c3 (set file), fn_3a6e2 (heap coalesce) + the allocator side of heap_Free, LORT Saturn-only code (VDP1 side).

## Level teardown and texture request path -- hand-named (10-10, twentieth pass)

- Textured filler table 0x15d050: each 0x50-byte row has 20 entries. The first 10 (+0x00..+0x24) are the far_TexPoly* fillers; the second 10 (+0x28..+0x4c, and the same halves of the rows at +0x78, +0xc8, +0x118) point at the fog-branch tails of those same fillers (0x159c50 = far_TexPoly8 fog tail: clamps the fog level to 15, patches 0x159cc9 / 0x159cc1 / 0x159cb3, jmp 0x157c89 far_EdgeWalk). They are not function starts and the rows are never selected (see the earlier note on the table). DumpDecomp of those addresses silently returns the containing filler. Candidate dropped.
- rend_CheckSortVals / rend_CheckSortValsAll were mis-described as debug checks. Renamed tex_RequestDrawn 0x16070 / tex_RequestDrawnAll 0x1602f: for every entry of the frame work-block list *DAT_000bf864 (stride 5 dwords) tex_Request(world+0xb0 slot, (entry[1]+0x14)+0x50 bigs, +0x4c obj, prio 15). This is the visibility -> texture request link that tex_StreamTick calls after eviction.
- level_Teardown 0x17b1c (eng_StartLevel, eng_PopLevelStack) order: spr_RemoveAll 0x18b29 (ctx+0x124 list, next +8), tex_FreeAll, level_FreeWorldIndex 0x17844 (ctx+0x38 / +0x3c), bigs_FreeAllStreams 0x17800 (ctx+0x140 list: [1] count, 0x10-byte entries from +8: +8 slot, +0xc bigs -> bigs_FreeStream 0x1773c), track_FreeRefArrays 0x1675f (ctx+0x44 instances, array +0xc), bigs_FreeStreamList 0x1764b (ctx+0x140), world_FreeAll 0x1daaf (ctx+0x20, next +0x10, world_Free), s_still_sub_links_12492(ctx+0x14c), level_FreeList24 0x110a5 (ctx+0x24 / +0x28, contents unknown), atab_FreeAll 0x173a7 (DAT_000a64a4 via list_Begin / list_Next), world_FreeSlots 0x16be9 (ctx+0x48 list [1] slot [2] next; frees loaded streams of the slot's bigs table ctx+0x40 + slot*0x14, then ctx+0x48 = ctx+0x4c = 0, FUN_0001550e), track_FreeInstances 0x16c91 (ring nodes next +0x10, instance next +0x14), eng_FreeLists(0x14), tex_FreePools 0x15ca2 (ctx+0x118, +0x10c, FUN_000154aa(ctx+0x110)), mem_FreeRaw ctx+0x40 / +0x38 (flags 3), +0x144 / +0xf8 / +0xfc (flags 1), level_FreeTables 0x20169 (ctx+0x74, +0x78, +0x64, +0x68, +0x6c, +0x70).
- bigs_FreeStream 0x1773c (slot, bigs): tex_FreeObject all objects, entry+0x30 = 0, mem_FreeRaw(entry, -3), mem_Free(entry+0x14, 3), resets the evict cursor ctx+0x11c when it pointed into the stream. tex_FreeWorldList 0x150ad (world list param, no callers): tex_FreeObject everything then tex_ClearQueue.
- Set file: set_LoadFile 0x1f4c3 (from level_LoadWorld after file_Load of setups\%s\worlds\%s\set%s.bin): wl3_SwapChunk(data), bswap32_at(data + *data) = entry count, set_SwapEntries 0x1f507 loops the entries: bswap32_at(p, p+4) size, wl3_SwapChunk(p), p += *p. A big-endian chunk list like the .wl3 (Saturn-authored).
- heap_Coalesce 0x3a6e2 (heap, block): merge with the next free block when block + size[+0xc] == next[+4]; called by heap_InsertFree.
- Gotcha: ApplyNames also renames the two old lines only after sed-deleting them from hand_scordos.txt (done: rend_CheckSortVals* lines removed). 481 hand names now; SCORWIN 135, LORT 34 after xalign.
- Next candidates: far_ProjectVertices 0x123637; FUN_0001550e / FUN_000154aa (slot-table and node-pool free helpers); what ctx+0x24 (level_FreeList24) and ctx+0x64..+0x78 (level_FreeTables) hold -- find their allocators in eng_StartLevel / level_LoadSetup; s_still_sub_links_12492; LORT Saturn-only code (VDP1 side).

## Vertex projection, palette tables, level allocators -- hand-named (10-10, twenty-first pass)

- far_ProjectVertices 0x123637 is now a Ghidra function (created by ApplyNames). Self-modifying: copies the 3x3 rotation (9 shorts at matrix) and the translation (entry+0x20 -> +8/+0xc/+0x10), the screen centre DAT_000bf85c / DAT_000bf860 and cam+0x24 into its own immediates (0x123730 .. 0x1237d5), patches the vertex base (verts+2 / +4 at 0x123720 / 0x123718). Per index (short list entry+0x10, count entry+0x14): v = verts[(idx & ~1) * 3] (3 shorts), rotate with scale = (world hdr (entry+8 -> +0x14 -> +0x28)) << 4 (mulhi), z clamped to >= 0xfa00 (near plane 64000), out[2] = z * k, w = round(cam+0x2c * 65536 / z), out[0] = x * w >> 32 + cx, out[1] = y * w >> 32 + cy; 3 shorts per vertex into cursor. Placeholders 0x12345678 / 0x5678 / 0x1234 in the decompile are the patched immediates.
- Palette tables (pal_BuildTables 0x20206 from eng_StartLevel, after level_FreeTables): in 8 bpp (ctx+0x100 == 8) ctx+0x64 = pal_BuildFadeTable 0x1f944 (FTAB, 16 x 256), +0x68 pal_BuildAddTable 0x1fa82 (ATAB 256x256 saturating add), +0x6c pal_BuildSubTable 0x1fb9f (STAB), +0x70 pal_BuildTintTable 0x1fccd (TTAB); otherwise ctx+0x74 rgb15_BuildFadeTable 0x1ff6c (16 x 32 shorts, value << 10) and +0x78 rgb15_BuildFadeTableRG 0x20044 (16 x 1024 shorts indexed r5<<5|g5). The .cmp file DAT_000bf9f0 is a 32x32x32 colour cube of palette indices: (r>>3)*0x400 + (g>>3)*0x20 + (b>>3). Level palette DAT_000bf9f4.
- ctx+0x24 / +0x28: level_AllocLoadList 0x11075 (n) = n x 8-byte LOADS list (contents still unknown; find the readers).
- Texture pools: tex_AllocPools 0x15bef (ctx+0x118 buckets 0x110 bytes, ctx+0x10c 500 nodes x 0x20 KELD2, ctx+0x110 pointer array KELD3, ctx+0x114 top); DAT_000a6508 = 500. tex_UpdateDepackBudget 0x14f73: DAT_000a650c = lerp_TableLookup 0x14f29 / lerp_Round 0x14ecf over thresholds DAT_000a6510 {0, 5000000, 11000000, 20000000} (free texture bytes DAT_000bfa28) -> rows {8000, 7000, 7000, 6000} DAT_000a6520.
- bigs_LoadAll 0x15880 -> bigs_LoadAllWorlds 0x158a1 (world index ctx+0x38, count +0x3c) -> bigs_LoadAllOfSlot 0x15749 (bigs_LoadStream every stream of the slot). Empty functions nop_1550e, nop_18c04.
- 500 hand names; SCORWIN 146, LORT 34 after xalign.
- Next candidates: readers of the LOADS list ctx+0x24; s_still_sub_links_12492 (ctx+0x14c setup links); s_REALLOCBLOCK_12cd0 / s_ok_d_12db9 / s_KELD4_16478 / s_SCRBUF_20bd9 in eng_StartLevel (the remaining string-named helpers there); the 8-bpp fillers that use FTAB/ATAB/STAB/TTAB (search for reads of ctx+0x64..+0x70); LORT Saturn-only code (VDP1 side).

## EXT checkpoint block (the "ReAlloc" system) -- hand-named (10-10, twenty-second pass)

- Every level-loaded struct is registered as a link on ctx+0x14c (ext_AddLink 0x12374 from level_LoadSetup per setup entry): 0x14-byte link [0] tag, [1] id, [2] sub-link list, [3] next, [4] prev; DAT_000bfa64[id] = link (ext_GetLink 0x12346). Sub-links (0x1c bytes: [0] id, [1] data, [2] size, [3] descriptor, [4] owner link, [5] next, [6] prev) are created by world_AllocSubLinked 0x12701 and removed by ext_RemoveSubLink 0x124fa / ext_RemoveLink 0x12483. Descriptor: +2 u16 struct size, +0xc signed byte pattern (negative = save n bytes, positive = skip n) = which fields of a struct are checkpointed.
- Ids: ext_NewId 0x12275 pops the free-id list DAT_000a6498 (list_Add / list_Begin / list_Next / list_Remove; stored +1) or DAT_000c0a64++ (max 0x400); while a block is being restored DAT_000c0a68 points at a -1-terminated short stream so the restored level gets the same ids ("Syncronize"). ext_ReleaseId 0x12323, ext_GetIdCount 0x12265. 0x111d8 is the generic list_Add (was atab_Register; the level block list DAT_000a64a4 is freed by atab_FreeAll).
- ext_BuildBlock 0x12cd0 (eng_StartLevel): size = ext_SizeLinkChunk 0x12bc4 + ext_SizeStructChunk 0x12c10 + ext_SizeFreeIdChunk 0x12ba9 + ext_SizeTimeChunk 0x12b99; ext_AllocHeader 0x1215c ([0] u16 total, [1] chunk count); ext_ReverseLinks 0x127cf; chunks via ext_BeginChunk 0x12199 ({tag, u16 len, u16 count}) + ext_AppendData 0x121d8 (4-byte rounded): RALL 0x52414c4c ext_AddLinkChunk 0x12957 {u16 count, u16 id, u32 tag, u16 sub ids, 0xffff}; STRU 0x53545255 ext_AddStructChunk 0x12a18 {u32 size, u32 id, bytes}; APTR 0x41505452 ext_AddFreeIdChunk 0x12898 {count, next id, free ids}; TIME 0x54494d45 ext_AddTimeChunk 0x12817 {ctx+0x30, ctx+0x130}; the finished block is copied into ctx+0x150 (the restart snapshot); ext_FreeBlock 0x12238. ext_Restore 0x12db9 (block) writes the STRU bytes back through the descriptors and restores TIME, DAT_000c0a68 = 0.
- level_Teardown_body 0x17b4d is the Ghidra split tail of level_Teardown. track_BuildObjectRefs 0x16478 (eng_StartLevel) builds the per-track-instance ref array [3] (KELD4, 0x14 per node: track position, obj, bigs, group length, group start) and stores each object's track distance at routdat+0x54 (vec_LocalToWorldPos -> s_SVIN_1e64e -> track_SegDistance); track_CountRingNodes 0x16431. vid_ClearBothPages 0x20bd9 (was s_SCRBUF) / vid_ClearAndFlip 0x20bc0.
- 528 hand names; SCORWIN 161, LORT 39 after xalign.
- Next candidates: s_SVIN_1e64e (track position from a world position) and track_SegDistance; world_AllocSubLinked 0x12701 (who registers what descriptors: find the descriptor tables); readers of the LOADS list ctx+0x24; the 8-bpp fillers reading ctx+0x64..+0x70; LORT Saturn-only code (VDP1 side).

## Track locate + runtime struct table (MAINENG.ASC loader) -- hand-named (10-10, twenty-third pass)
Names in `ghidra/hand_scordos.txt` (names_add23, 36 lines; 562 scordos functions named, SCORWIN 172, LORT 42 via xalign).
- **track_LocatePos 0x1e64e** (was s_SVIN): (trk, rec) finds the track segment under a world position. Segment list from trk+0x74
  (+0x1c, +4; 0x48-byte nodes, first DAT_000c0b98 / end DAT_000c0b94), starts from the cached segment trk+0x38 - 1 (3 steps only when
  the distance < 640000). Test per segment: track_SegPlaneDist 0x1e536 (dot(pos - seg+4, seg+0x10) via mulhi32) flips sign between
  seg and next, and track_SegLineDistSq 0x1e57a (direction seg+0x30, fixmul) is minimal. Writes trk+0x40/+0x44 (the two plane
  distances, abs), trk+0x38 = segment, rec+8..+0x10 = point on the track (lerp_Weighted 0x1e60d, float), rec+0x48 = lateral distance
  (vec_Length). sys_Fatal "SVIN" when no segment. Callers: track_BuildObjectRefs, car_UpdateSoundEmitter, insrestarts_Init, fn_3860d, fn_389d6.
- **The game parses MAINENG.ASC at runtime.** struct_LoadTable 0x11f7a (engine boot; Ghidra split tail 0x12000): file_Load
  `d:\wincode\h\maineng.asc` -> text_CountLines 0x11bae -> struct_ParseList 0x1193f (recursive descent: lines with a ',' are
  "name offset, size, flag"; flag 1 = struct line, 2 = field; constants without ',' are skipped; a struct's fields are parsed into its
  child list +0x10 with the struct name as terminator) -> struct_ReverseSymLinks 0x11b73 (file order) -> struct_BuildPattern 0x11830
  per struct (struct_EmitRuns 0x1172b emits signed run bytes: negative = copy n bytes, positive = skip n, flushed in steps of +-0x7c;
  sum of |runs| must equal the struct size, sys_Fatal with the struct name; stored in a SYMFST buffer at node+0xc) ->
  struct_FreeFieldLists 0x115f4 -> struct_PackTable 0x11ef4 (one SYMBOLBLK: [0] max struct size, [1] count, [2] packed node list,
  [3] SYMLNTAB descriptor array at +0x10; struct_PackedSize 0x11d78, struct_PackNodes 0x11df1) -> struct_FreeSymLinkTree 0x1161c.
  DAT_000bfa60 = the packed table; struct_FreeTable 0x12082 frees it (eng_CloseDown); struct_CheckTable 0x11be5 debug.
- SYMLINK node (struct_NewSymLink 0x114f7, 0x1c + name): +0 offset, +2 size, +4 flag, +6 index, +8 name ptr, +0xc pattern,
  +0x10 child list, +0x14 next, +0x18 prev, +0x1c name text. struct_ParseValues 0x11653 (string site 0x116b2) reads the 3 numbers;
  struct_FreeSymLink 0x11547 ("FreeSymLink still has SubLinks"); free variants 0x115b7 / 0x1162d.
- Lookup: struct_FindIndex 0x12099 (stricmp over DAT_000bfa60[2], returns index + 1, sys_Fatal > 0x5ab), struct_GetDescriptor 0x120ff
  (DAT_000bfa60[3][index - 1], "no struct def"). ext_AddSubLink 0x123e6 (was s_REALLOCSUBLINK) registers a struct instance for the
  EXT checkpoint: 0x1c-byte sub-link [0] id ext_NewId [1] data [2] size [3] descriptor [4] owner [5] next [6] prev; DAT_000bfa64[id].
  world_AllocSubLinked 0x12701 = mem_AllocRaw(size + 4) + ext_AddSubLink(hdr+0xa4, out, size, struct_FindIndex(typetag)).
  So the EXT save pattern (which bytes of a struct are checkpointed) comes straight from the MAINENG.ASC field list: the runtime
  struct table is the same text we imported into Ghidra (ImportMainengStructs.java). The PC maineng.asc differs from the Saturn one
  in 42 lines (alloc structs) -- a mismatch would change the checkpoint layout, not the code.
- Text helpers (Watcom-side): text_LineEnd 0x3c8d2 (CR/LF/NUL set at 0xa3de5), text_FindAnyOf 0x3c716 (n < 0 = first char NOT in
  the set, 0x3c635), text_NextLine 0x3c922 (LF/NUL set 0xa3de8), wat_memicmp 0x40b59. ext_SetIdCount 0x12255 (DAT_000c0a64 = n,
  level_LoadSetup passes 0), ext_GetIdCount2 0x1226b.
- Gotcha: a hand line must point at the function entry; the string-site address 0x116b2 gave "no function" and the line was moved to 0x11653.
- Next candidates: struct_EmitRuns 0x1172b + struct_PackNodes 0x11df1 (confirm the run encoding), track_SegDistance 0x1e383 detail,
  the 8-bpp fillers reading ctx+0x64..+0x70 (grep the filler dumps for 0x64/0x68/0x6c/0x70), readers of the LOADS list ctx+0x24,
  car_UpdateSoundEmitter / insrestarts_Init (track_LocatePos users), LORT Saturn-only code (VDP1 side).

## Struct save patterns, track frame + follow camera -- hand-named (10-10, twenty-fourth pass)
Names in `ghidra/hand_scordos.txt` (names_add24, 23 lines).
- **MAINENG.ASC flag column decides what the checkpoint saves.** struct_EmitRuns 0x1172b: flag 2..0xff = saved field (run -= size,
  after flushing a positive skip run in 0x7c steps), flag 0 or >= 0x100 = skipped (run += size after flushing a negative copy run in
  0x84 steps), flag 1 = the struct line. Flags in the PC file: 2 (747 fields), 1 (109 structs), 256 / 257 / 258 / 513 / 514
  (pointers and runtime data, e.g. InsFP_InsRout, _ConRouts_RoutPtr, ObjRAMExtHeader_OECdsPtr, AnimRam_AnimPtrs). Pattern byte:
  negative = copy n, positive = skip n. struct_PackNodes 0x11df1 / struct_PackedSize 0x11d78 pack the 0x1c node + name + pattern.
- **Track frame.** track_InterpFrame 0x1e885 (after track_LocatePos) lerps between the segment trk+0x38 and the next with the two
  plane distances: trk+0 tangent (3 shorts, from the segment's first 3 bytes), trk+0x14 / +0x18 = seg+0x1c / +0x20 = **track tube
  radii** (y / x), trk+0x20 normal (seg+0x10), trk+0x2c direction (seg+0x30). track_BuildBasis 0x1eaa6 = 3x3 basis at trk+0x50
  (normal, cross, -cross) so x87_Store3Ints(trk+0x50, v) = world -> track space and vec_RotateStore3 = back.
  track_ProjectPos 0x23a52: progress = trk+0x1c * 0x10000 / 0x3e800, lane = d.x * 0x10000 / 30000 + 0x200000 clamped 0..0x3f0000,
  height = -d.y. insrestarts_Init 0x275be: MPOS chunk -> STMARKS {ptr, x, y, z, u16 lane, u16 progress - DAT_000c0cf4 mod track length}.
- **Follow camera (moincam).** State DAT_000c6138: [0..2] pos, [3..5] velocity (3/4 damping), [6..7] last radii, [9..0xb] smoothed
  track point, [0xc..0xe] up (tangent * 0x8000 - obj+0x68 * 0x800), [0xf..0x11] track point, [0x12..0x1a] matrix.
  moincam_UpdateTrackPos 0x3860d (player pos -> trk+0x78 progress / +0x7c lane / +0x80 height, ctx+0x108 = camera track distance),
  moincam_FollowTick 0x389d6, moincam_ClampToTrack 0x38d8d (ellipse rx = trk+0x18, ry = trk+0x14 via trig_Atan2 0x101ae,
  trig_Cos 0x10161, vec_Length2D 0x102a9; pulls the camera back inside the tube, 0xa000 pull when the tube narrows),
  moincam_SnapInsideTrack 0x38c81, moincam_BuildMatrix 0x38744 (vec_Cross 0x1036c, player roll PTR_DAT_000a64b8+0xdc through
  vec_RotateByAngles 0x10483), moincam_Reset 0x3893c (obj+0x98 byte+1 & 2 = snap), moincam_PublishView 0x386f3 (view record
  PTR_DAT_000a86d4: +4 pos, +0x1c matrix, flags |= 3). All called from moincam_RateLoop.
- Next candidates: FUN_000222f2 (tail of track_ProjectPos), moincam_RateLoop itself and the other camera modes (freecam_Init /
  endseqcam_Init users of world_AllocSubLinked), FUN_00021fd1 / FUN_00024a04 / FUN_00025559 (vec_Cross callers), the 8-bpp fillers
  reading ctx+0x64..+0x70, readers of the LOADS list ctx+0x24, LORT Saturn-only code (VDP1 side).

## Camera modes: anchors, chase <-> in-car blend, look-at -- hand-named (10-10, twenty-fifth pass)
- 598 scordos names (SCORWIN 199, LORT 43 via xalign). names_add25.txt: 19 lines, none replaced.
- Camera object = a normal world object: +0x20 eye, +0x2c committed eye, +0x38 committed matrix, +0x5c current matrix, +0x98 -> subject record (+1 flags: bit1 snap, +4..+0xc position, +0x18..+0x20 velocity, +0x24 track struct). cam_StoreViewSnapshot 0x37654 commits when the eye moves > 0x3e800 or view flag 0x20.
- Camera target record (first entry of the world+0x84 list): +0 flags (bit0 = in-car / eye near subject), +0xc snap frame counter (camtarget_SetSnapFrames 0x39309 sets 4), +0x14 eye, +0x20 look point, +0x44 eye anchor rec, +0x64 look anchor rec. Anchor rec (8 dwords): [0] height offset, [1] back offset, [2] speed lo, [3] speed hi, [4] along-track weight, [5..7] last world point. camtarget_SmoothAnchor 0x39318 blends in track space with w = clamp((|vz| - lo) / (hi - lo)); moincam_ComputeAnchors 0x39499 drives both.
- Chase <-> in-car: CameraRAM+0xec requested view (0 chase), +0xf0 current, DAT_000c613c blend 0..0x10000 (0x10000 = in-car). moincam_ModeSwitch 0x39261, moincam_ModeBlendStep 0x39087 (t += t/8 capped 0x1800 per tick; mat_Lerp; in-car offset (0, -0xe100, -0x7d00) via cam_ViewFromSubject 0x38fd0). Readers of DAT_000c613c: FUN_00031b6b, s_WBG_32a1f (not read yet).
- Roll: cam_SubjectRollAngle 0x384f8 (up (0,0x4000,0) rotated by CameraRAM+0x44/+0x48, vec_ToLocal, atan2), cam_SmoothRoll 0x38563 (speed factor clamp(|v| - 0x73a0, 1..0x15ae0)/0x15ae0, step * 0xe00 clamped +-0x38e); moincam_ComputeAnchors then forces CameraRAM+0xdc = 0, so roll is disabled in this build.
- mat_LookAt 0x379f5 (vec_DirToAngles 0x10795 -> mat_SetEuler with CameraRAM+0x40); cam_ApplyViewOption 0x3810c copies DAT_000a666c (0..2, written by FUN_00034099 at 0x3417d / 0x344a1) into CameraRAM+0xf4; moincam_PublishTarget 0x386b5; cam_BuildDepthTable 0x2163d (DEPTHTAB DAT_000c0cd0, 2000 bytes, ramp 0..0x7f from CameraRAM+0x1c>>16 to 0x400).
- Hand lines added for freecam_Init 0x381c9, moincam_RateLoop 0x396f1 (intro camera DAT_000c0cd4 == 1: DAT_000c0c10 * 0x800 blend; DAT_000c0c14 = velocity . normal), endseqcam_Init 0x3a28a; nop_222f2, nop_2a6c9.
- Next candidates: FUN_00034099 (view option / key handler), FUN_00031b6b + s_WBG_32a1f (DAT_000c613c readers), camwpul_RateLoop 0x37a65 (the other camera mode), vec_Cross callers FUN_00021fd1 / FUN_00024a04 / FUN_00025559, the 8-bpp fillers reading ctx+0x64..+0x70, readers of the LOADS list ctx+0x24 and of DEPTHTAB DAT_000c0cd0, LORT Saturn-only code (VDP1 side).

## Menu system, CD audio, world-pull camera; 103 missed functions -- hand-named (10-10, twenty-sixth pass)
- chkscan.py (scratchpad): lists `push n; call __CHK` prologues (6A xx E8 / 68 xxxxxxxx E8 targeting __CHK 0x40a8a or 0x20d1c) not covered by any funcinfo function. Found 106; ApplyNames created 103 as `unk_<addr>` (3 inside existing bodies: 0x111b5, 0x327f8, 0x39e88 dropped). Leaf functions without __CHK can still hide in gaps.
- refscan gotcha: a reference inside a gap is attributed to the nearest preceding function. The DAT_000a666c writes at 0x3417d / 0x344a1 belong to menu_ViewOptionsPage 0x3413d (not FUN_00034099), the DAT_000c613c read at 0x330ec to menu_LevelSelectPage 0x32e40 (not s_WBG).

## Mid-instruction function entries removed -- FixSplitEntries.java (10-10)
Ghidra had 144 unreferenced functions in scordos.bin whose entry lay 1..8 bytes after the fall-through of the preceding instruction (e.g. FUN_00024a04 = "and al, 0x54" inside car_ApplySteerYaw's `mov [esp+0x54], eax` at 0x24a02; FUN_00025559 inside car_ApplySurfaceNormal at 0x25557; FUN_0002a48c inside bgexpl_Coli). New script `ghidra\scripts\FixSplitEntries.java` (arg `list` = dry run) removes them, clears the listing from the fall-through to the end of the bogus body and re-disassembles; then RepairBodies.java twice: merged 29 + 18 fragments, grown 161 + 10, functions 2417 -> 2276, code bytes 344956 -> 364731. Hand names kept (ApplyNames 733). xalign improved: 751 pairs, SCORWIN 234, LORT 46. Pipeline order now: FindWatcomFunctions -> FixSplitEntries -> RepairBodies (until 0 / 0). Merged this way: car_ApplySteerYaw 0x247ba now 823 bytes, car_ApplySurfaceNormal 0x254a0 now 493, anim_Visible 0x2e6e7 now 287 (calls FUN_00021fd1 = the per-polygon normal builder). Gotcha: `py -3.13 -I dis.py ../pc/flat/scordos.bin 0xaddr:n` (without -I the scratchpad dis.py shadows the stdlib `dis` module and capstone fails to import).

## Pass 40 (10-10): the DOS sound library is the Miles Sound System (MSS 3.x)

- The library below snd_Init / snd_Shutdown is MSS 3.x (Miles / AIL). Every public AIL_* wrapper debug-logs its own name string (136 strings at 0xa46e0..0xa59a8, listed in the scratchpad ailstr.txt; the string-to-function map is ailjoin.txt / ailinner.txt), so the wrappers were named automatically: 124 wrappers whose function existed + 12 whose Ghidra entry was missing (true starts by prologue search: AIL_interrupt_divisor 0x47d20, AIL_set_sample_loop_block 0x46740, AIL_install_DIG_driver_image 0x45e00, AIL_sample_position 0x457f0, AIL_register_EOB_callback 0x455d0, AIL_install_MDI_INI 0x44fd0, AIL_sequence_volume 0x44320, AIL_XMIDI_master_volume 0x44060, AIL_register_prefix_callback 0x43370, AIL_true_sequence_channel 0x427e0, AIL_redbook_open 0x42340, AIL_redbook_close 0x42290; their split pieces FUN_00047d83 / 45ee8 / 45881 / 45891 / 45684 / 45082 / 44381 / 440c7 / 433ed / 4289e are to be merged by RepairBodies). The wrapper pattern: DAT_000d3e54++ (API nesting depth), DAT_000d3e58 (debug log on), DAT_000d3e5c (MSS_SYS_DEBUG: log nested calls too), FUN_0004a055 (6 B) check, FUN_00048dd0 (286 B) logs the name, the single inner call = AIL_API_<name>, FUN_0004a2e7 + FUN_00049150 (int result) / FUN_00049100 (pointer result) log the result; DAT_000bdef4 = AIL started, DAT_000bdef0 = once-only init done, DAT_000d3e34 = debug file name, DAT_000d3e60 = start timer.
- Inner implementations named AIL_API_<name> (123, one callee per wrapper after removing the debug helpers): AIL_API_startup 0x4d180 (calls AIL_API_set_preference 0x4d6e0 21 times = default preferences), AIL_API_shutdown 0x4d150, AIL_API_install_DIG_INI 0x4e840, AIL_API_allocate_sample_handle 0x4e770, AIL_API_uninstall_DIG_driver 0x4e820, AIL_API_set_digital_master_volume 0x4e2b0, AIL_API_end_sample 0x4e390.
- snd_Init is therefore: AIL_startup, AIL_install_DIG_INI (0 = ok), AIL_set_preference x3, 16 x AIL_allocate_sample_handle, semitone table by rt_pow 0x49252 (Watcom pow), AIL_set_digital_master_volume; snd_StopAll = AIL_end_sample per channel; snd_Shutdown = AIL_uninstall_DIG_driver + AIL_shutdown. rt_getenv 0x4cfc0 = Watcom getenv (MSS_DEBUG / MSS_SYS_DEBUG).

- Open items done (10-10 late): (1) Zyrinx / Scavenger Saturn list, verified from Wikipedia (Scavenger, Inc.; Lemon (developer); Amok), GameSpot "Scavenger Tells Its Tale" and unreleasedgames.miraheze "Into the Shadows": Scavenger's 1996 GT Interactive deal was four titles: Scorcher (Zyrinx, PC 1996 + Saturn 1997; announced as Vertigo, grew out of the Zyrinx 32X engine), Amok (Lemon, Saturn + DOS/Windows Oct 1996; Japan Saturn by Koei; PlayStation version cancelled; began as a 32X demo), Into the Shadows (Triton, later Starbreeze; Saturn/PC, never released, cancelled 1997-98) and 4X Frenzy / Mudkicker (monster-truck racer, PS1/PC, cancelled). Scavenger bankrupt 1998 (GT dispute, $1.9M award to Scavenger in 2000); Zyrinx + Lemon people formed Reto-Moto 1998 (IO Interactive). So the only other shipped Scavenger Saturn game is AMOK (Lemon): same era, same CD library likely (GFS_SBL), and its PC build is DOS + Windows like Scorcher (Watcom to verify when the files are in hand; the AMOKDEMO on the Scorcher PC disc is a first sample). Zyrinx's own earlier games Sub-Terrania and Red Zone are Mega Drive 68k (different toolchain, same team). (2) Published: public repo https://github.com/indieindie7/scorcher-decomp (commit 650c221, 47 files: NOTES.md, hand_*.txt, funcinfo_*.tsv, xalign/sig/restore, x86watcom.cspec, scripts/*.java, ghidra/decomp/*.c, tools/*.py, README with the DMCA-will-not-be-contested line, MIT for scripts/tools only; no game files, .gitignore blocks gpr/rep/bin/exe/iso/logs/extracted/pc/saturn). - Native build pass 3 (10-10): first headless run (build/run1, --vblanks 1800): boot /0 -> program start INTRO 00280000 -> LORT 06004000 -> overlay CDCON 00200000 (from pr 0600615A) all automatic; then FATAL at 7.087 s "interrupt 43: handler 0600AABC did not rte (went to 0600B710)" after 90 good vector-43 interrupts. Cause = the game's own background task: handler 0600AABC jumps through the hook *0x06016AB4 to 0600B850, which (if *0x06016AC0 == 0 and *0x06016ABC == 0x4B4C4152 "KLAR") saves r15 into 0x06016AC0, pushes SR 0 + PC 0600B710 and rte's INTO the task 0600B710 (runs with interrupts open; ends with its own rte at 0600B7E8 back to the interrupted code). saturnkit's sat_interrupt (saturnkit/runtime/machine.cpp ~line 346) treated that as fatal. Patch (local saturnkit clone, generic, not game knowledge): after the handler returns, while c.pc != kIntPC and c.pc is an entry, sh2_call(c, c.pc) nested in the interrupt.
- Native build, step 6 (10-10 23:45): after the MODR fix LORT runs to 7.087 s then FATAL "interrupt 43: handler 0600AABC did not rte (went to 0600B710)". 0600AABC = the VBlank-OUT handler: saves all regs on the stack, ++*0x26016AA4, then jmp @(*0x06016AB4) = the callback 0600B710 (leaf, no prologue; seeded with LORT=...@06004000+0600B710, LORT then 826 functions, same fatal). 0600B710 ends in a task switch: at 0600B788 r1 = *0x06016AC0 (a saved stack pointer); if nonzero -> 0600B7B0 mov r1,r15; *06016AC0 = 0; *06016ABC = 1; pops the regs and rte (resumes another context); else (*0x2600B9DC != 0 -> .word FFFF trap) *06016ABC = 1; jsr 0600B8D8 (should not return on hardware?); then *06016ABC = 0x4B4C4152 "KLAR" and bra self (0600B7AC) forever. Runtime sat_interrupt (machine.cpp:332-350) restores the saved SH2Context after a handler, so a stack switch by rte is undone; saturnkit's landings (discover.py:748 _landings, emit.py:21, machine.cpp:376 SH2Unwind) cover longjmp returns only. Open: read 0600B8D8 and the function that stores 06016AC0; decide between a runtime change (keep the context the handler switched to) and a hook. The race-load fatal: at 51.819 s the slave job pointer 26016A98 = 06012908 (not 0600B370), and that indirect call returns to 0600603E with r15 060D2F54 -> 060D2F58 (+4, one word over-popped). 06012908 = hand asm (no hand name yet): pushes r14..r0 (15) + r6, checks *(+16)->+8 == 0x5046; mismatch -> bra 06012E88 with r6 still pushed; rts at 060129CA/06012A7E/06012AE6/06012B7A/06012BB0/06012D48/06012E48, `add #4,r15` at 06012C88 (delay slot of bra 060129E4 at 06012C86). Next: find which exit the C++ took (the emitted duplicated tails), compare with hardware stack depth.
- Native build pass 6c (10-11, stopped for the night): job 06012908 is called ONCE, at 51.819 s, with r0 = 0020AEB4 (just past CDCON's image end 0x0020AE7C = CDCON BSS); words there 07D40000 04000000 00410101 00001BBA = CD status data (same as the CD cmd 00 replies 0400 4101 0100 1BBA in the log), [r0+16] = 0, so the 'PF' (0x5046) header test fails and the bad path (bra 060129E0 -> 06012E88 at stack depth 16 instead of 17) over-pops one word -> handler 06006000 rte's to 000000E0. So the real bug is upstream: the slave job slot 26016A98 / its argument gets 06012908 + a CDCON pointer, i.e. someone queued the wrong job/argument during the race load (CD read path?). Next: find who stores 06012908 into 26016A98 (scan LORT.BIN for the literal 06012908) and where the argument comes from (handler reads *0x260060A4 into r0 before jsr), compare with what the hardware would do. Debug code still in: core.cpp (job06012908 note + indirect r15 check + #include saturn.h), machine.cpp (irqdbg + sh2_stack_mismatch), sh2.h SH2_CHK, services_stub.cpp stub; build/recomp sed-patched (regenerate to clean).
- Native build pass 7 (10-11 morning): race-load fatal FIXED. Cause: the VDP1 draw-end handler 0600AB4C (reads COPR, a command with CMDCTRL bit 14 = a split point; rewrites it to 0x5000 jump-to-next, then queues job 06012908 with r0 = the VRAM command pointer) stores the job in 26016A98, writes SINIT 21000000 at 0600ABC4 and only THEN the argument into 260060A4 at 0600ABC8 (0600B524 does the same at 0600B580/0600B584). The runtime's slave_kick handed the slave the CPU at once, so the slave read a stale argument (0020AEB4). Fix: saturnkit/runtime/machine.cpp slave_kick only sets the capture flag + g_slave_idle = false; the slave runs from the master's next poll (master_poll_devices). build
un17 (4800 VBlanks, race script, shots 3100..4800): no fatal, 5025 draws, 2849 frame changes, sound on; shot-4000/4800 = in race (LAP 1/4 POS 4/4, timer 30 -> 19, sky + HUD) but the track, ground and cars are NOT drawn (black below the horizon) = next problem. The 'stack: cpu 1, call returning to 0601206C moved r15 060D2D64 -> 060D22B4' notes are octree_CollectFaces's intended 0xAB0 lowering. Debug code still in (see pass 6c).
- Native build pass 8 (10-11): debug code removed (core.cpp, machine.cpp, sh2.h, services_stub.cpp back to clean + the slave_kick fix), build/recomp wiped and regenerated (stale p_zero_*.cpp from the other chat's ZERO= emit deleted; not in CMakeLists), full ninja build incl. selftest OK. hand_LORT.txt 0x06005ee8 renamed slave_Restart. VDP2 RBG1 added in saturnkit/runtime/vdp2.cpp: setup_rbg(r, which); RBG1 = parameter B with NBG0's registers (CHCTLA low byte 0x28, PNCN0 0x30, BMPNA 0x2C, PRINA 0xF8, CCRNA 0x108, CCCTL/CLOFEN/CLOFSL bit 0, CRAOFA 0xE4, N0TPON bgon bit 8), shown in NBG0's slot, NBG0-3 off while it is on; RBG0 keeps parameter A only when R1ON. Race BGON = 0x1130 (R0ON + R1ON). build
un18 (4000 VBlanks): dead-tree horizon (RBG1) now drawn above the sky; the ground below the horizon is still black. Next: --dump 4000 (layout: VDP1 VRAM 0x80000, draw FB, VDP1 regs, VDP2 VRAM 0x80000, CRAM 0x1000, VDP2 regs 0x200) to see if the floor is RBG0 (coefficient table) or VDP1 polygons.

- Native build pass 9 (10-11, in progress): the race picture has sky (RBG0), the RBG1 tree horizon and the HUD; the track, ground and cars are missing. The VDP1 chunk chain WORKS: per frame the draws are 18-20 commands (frame change, PTMR 2), then the PTMR=1 restarts give 0, 0, 4 and 10 commands (about 33 commands in total, all type 2 distorted sprites with PMOD 008B / 0088 / 00A8 = probably the HUD). The split markers are C601 / C002 (END + bit 14), records whose flag +2 has bit 0x80 set, from vdp1_BuildCmdList 0600CE6C. Each restart advances cmd0 = 5000 jump to COPR+4 (one slot past the split). Job 06012908 (the draw-end job, r0 = the split command) checks *(cmd+16)+8 == 'PF' 0x5046, culls by the command's +12/+14 and +20/+22 against x -160..160 and y -112..112, and SOFTWARE-DRAWS into the VDP1 framebuffer at 05C9C140 (the centre, 0x1C140 = row 112, x 160). It does: about 700 CPU framebuffer word writes per frame (values 8000 / FFFF / A529 ...), from the slave (pr 0600603E), at small top-of-screen areas (offsets 0x2A32.., 0x2E22.., 0x3218..). So the chunk mechanism is fine and the 3D scene itself is nearly empty (the render list holds about 5 PF packets). A 300 us deferred-draw experiment (env SATURNKIT_VDP1_DELAY) changed nothing. Debug code in vdp1.cpp: env SATURNKIT_VDP1_LIST (per-command trace + fbw trace after draw 1960), SATURNKIT_VDP1_DELAY, g_dbg_fbw count in the draw trace line; remove it when done. The trace is in build/run21/trace.txt (3300 VBlanks). Next: why the scene is empty (the camera / object list / bigs streaming / octree culling), starting from rend_TransformAllMeshes 0600DB1E (mesh count *060FCB0C) and the draw-record list in vdp1_BuildCmdList (PTR_DAT_0600d080 + 0x2c .. + 0x30).
- Pass 9 DONE (10-11): ROOT CAUSE was the recompiler, not the emulation (mac.l / mul.l checked correct). The load at 0600D974 `mov.l @(0x0600D9F4),r2` was folded to a constant 0: 0600D9F4 is a literal-pool slot holding the far-clip distance, written at run time through the base 0600D9E8 (+12) loaded as a literal at 0600D7AE / 0600D91E / 0600DA7A. With far = 0 the test `cmp/ge r3,r2` at 0600D978 (radius<<5 + far >= depth) culled every mesh. Fix in saturnkit/recomp/__main__.py volatile_literals(): a literal value that points at data (not code) also marks every pool slot in v..v+62 volatile (mov.l @(disp,Rn) reach). After regen the load is `ld32(0x0600D9F4u)` (p_lort_001.cpp:6110). vdp1.cpp debug (SATURNKIT_VDP1_LIST / _DELAY, g_dbg_fbw) removed. build/run22 (4000 VBlanks, race input as run17): mesh count *060FCB0C = 5, shot-3400 shows the textured track, tunnel, walls, car and HUD.
- Native build pass 10 (10-11): driving works. (1) Holding B from VBlank 3000 hit FATAL '1-byte read of 00300D12' on the slave: lldb (C:/Program Files/LLVM/bin/lldb.exe, `b sat_fatal`, `bt`) gave f_060078EC <- f_060F3094 (slave job loop) <- f_06005F20; 060078EC is one of the sys_InstallStreamHandlers software sprite scalers (060079E8 mov.b @(r0,r5),r0: r5 = texture + u>>16, r0 = (v>>16)*width) reading past the end of low WRAM. Fix: runtime/mmio.cpp area_of 0x00300000-0x003FFFFF 'nothing past low work RAM' reads 0 (run25: 4152 reads, 00300CCF..00302650). (2) Speed: headless 4600 VBlanks took 119.8 s wall (0.64x); lldb sample = both threads in give() pthread_cond_wait. runtime/machine.cpp give() now uses an atomic turn + wait_turn() spinning 4000 pauses before the condvar (g_sleepers): 48.7 s (1.58x overall, race 3000-4600 1.37x), shots bit-identical (run25 vs run27 shot-4600 md5 5472ddf4...). Paced windowed run30 to 4200: 70.7 s wall for 70.1 s game, audio 1 times empty. Launcher native/play-scorcher.bat (borderless fullscreen, F11/Alt+Enter).
- Pass 10 cont.: build/run31 (14000 VBlanks, B held from 3000, steering pulses; buttons in --input join with '+', e.g. 5000:B+LEFT) has no fatal: race, the checkpoint timer hits 00 near VBlank 6100 (time over), title screen (8000), attract demo renders (14000). saturnkit diff refreshed into github/scorcher-decomp/native/saturnkit-patches.diff (not committed).
- Pass 9 cont.: the 3D scene is empty because rend_CollectVisibleMeshes 0600D6F6 passes 0 of the 14 mesh entries (render block 060720A8 [0] = 0xE; the mesh count *060FCB0C stays 0 and is only reset by 0600D6A4). All entries take the 0600D8B6 face test (flag +12 bit 2 set): the camera from CameraRAM (*060FCBA8)+0x80/+0x84/+0x88 gives r6 00000100 r10 00016A0B r9 0001BE56, constant over frames 3290-3300. The face test at 0600D91C: the vertex bytes << 8 minus the local camera 0600D9E8, dot with the normal bytes (mul.l) >= 0, then the frustum checks with clrmac + 3x mac.l @r0+,@r1+ against the plane table 0600D9C4 and cmp/pl mach. Suspect: the recompiler's mac.l (64-bit accumulate / the S-bit saturation) or mul.l. Debug instrumentation: build/recomp/p_lort_001.cpp has MESH/FACE sat_note lines (backup build/p_lort_001.cpp.bak; regenerate to clean).

## Native build pass 11 (10-11): music "two tracks at once" (in progress)
- No CD-DA at the title/menu. Music = SCSP + 68000 driver from SCORSAT/GAME/SATMUSIC/TITLE.MUS (404700 b). The MUS is a full sound RAM image in 3 segments: file 0x24..0x6000 -> RAM 0 (68000 vectors: SSP 0xA000, PC 0x17C4, level-1 autovector 0x154A); file 0x6000..0x59000 -> RAM 0x2C240..0x7F240 (sample bank); file 0x59000..0x62CDC -> RAM 0xAF6C. Load verified byte-identical (run32/dump-1490.bin, sound RAM appended TEMP to --dump).
- Instrument table at RAM 0x2C000 (word offset list 0028 003A 0044 ...), records ~0x20 b. Record SA field +0x2C000 = slot SA, e.g. 0x0022A92C -> SA 0x5692C (16-bit, LSA 0 LEA 0x5145, at 0x2C074), 0x00121CB4 -> SA 0x4DCB4 (8-bit, LSA 0A7E LEA 1330, at 0x2C104). Bit 0x200000 seems = 16-bit.
- Key-on trace (run32/keys.txt, --trace): title/menu plays ONE voice at a time, a new slot every 0.2 s (slots 0,8,16,24,1,9,...), previous keyed off ~6 ms later, all on SA 0x5692C at FNS 10A/2BA/38D/000 (a chord arpeggio); from 32 s also SA 0x4DCB4. No DSP output, no EXTS. Sample 0x5692C decodes as smooth loud 16-bit BE (std 26565) = a pre-mixed loop, not a note.
- Other table instruments (e.g. 0x003004D2, 0x00309CBA) are never keyed. Suspect the driver (68000 / timers / mailbox) not the SCSP mixer.
- Driver (68000 code in RAM 0x1000..0x6000; disasm via capstone M68K into build/run32/drv.asm): entry 0x1000 waits for 'SEGA' at RAM 0x5D6C; a6 = 0x7000 vars, a5 = 0x100000 SCSP. Main loop polls SCIPD bit 6 (timer A, reload $418 = 0xA7, ~2 ms) and calls 0x128E / 0x116A; timer B IRQ (level-2 autovector, handler 0x154A) reloads $41A = 0x1D4, adds 0xC0 to var 0x881A, ++0x8838, calls 0x4308. Both measured at ~500 Hz (dumps 1440/1500). Command slots: RAM 0x700..0x7F0, 16 bytes each, byte 0 = command (dispatch 0x17F2: >=0x80 table at 0x180E, <0x20 second table); driver zeroes the word when done. Other mailbox bytes 0x483 (song?), 0x4E0/0x4E1.
- SH-2 writes to sound RAM < 0x2C000 after VBlank 1000: only the clear + MUS load at 19.5 s, then 0x730 = 0x0500 (cmd 5), 0x742 = 0x0C, 0x740 = 0x8200 (cmd 0x82) at 19.586 s; nothing more through VBlank 2300 (menu). No CD-DA in a windowed run either (run33; audio stream 58 times empty).
- Instrument records (0x24 b, e.g. 0x2C076): +0xC long = r[0] low bits << 16 | SA low (0x0022A92C: LPCTL 1, 16-bit, SA 0x2A92C + bank 0x2C000), +0x10 LSA, +0x12 LEA, +0x22 e0xx root note. 0x5692C is the only 16-bit sample; the slot values match the data exactly.
- No SH-2 sound RAM writes at all after VBlank 1250 (no streaming). CD block: only data reads (track01 FADs) through the menu; no track02 play. Sample 0x5692C: smooth 16-bit, spectral flatness 0.68 (noise/drum-loop like, not a tonal note), 0.94 s at 22 kHz.
- Routine 0x4CB6 reads each slot's CA via MSLC/monitor 0x408 and reports changed slots to the SH-2 (status record at *(0x412), MCIPD bit 5 via $42A/$42C). 0x4A8E caller; EXTS/EFREG levels also copied out (0x4A40..).
- Hypothesis: the 4 tracker channels (slots n*8+k) all get instrument record 5 (0x2C076, the 16-bit loop) instead of their own; or cmd 5 at 0x730 is a jingle/SFX start. Next: decode the command tables at 0x180E / after 0x1854 and the note-on path that picks the instrument (grep drv.asm for lea $2c000 / instrument table).
- Driver = standard Sega SDDRVS layout: area map at sound RAM 0x500 (8-byte entries): seq bank at 0x00B000 size 0x9706; DSP prog 0x0171CA size 0x540; DSP RAM 0x018000 size 0x11000; tone bank 0x02C000 size 0x532D4. Host command blocks 0x700+0x10*n.
- Title song (seq bank 0xB000, 1 song at +6: reso 0x180, tempo 400000 us = 150 BPM, data 0xB01E): notes per MIDI ch 0..12 = 143,132,77,61,390,428,346,12,18,666,548,543,73; program change ch n -> voice n (0..15). So 13 channels should sound; we only hear voice 5 (+ the 8-bit 0x4DCB4 later): the driver drops most note-ons.
- Driver key-on path: 0x2CDE voice lookup, slot allocator 0x2CF0-0x2E0C (free list at 0x78C0, busy flag byte +0x34 of 0x40-byte slot records at 0x8000, counters 0x8820/0x8822/0x882A/0x8824), KYONB loop 0x2E12, KYONEX at 0x2E6A (read slot 0 byte 0, OR 0x10). Seq event read 0x4D1A, player tick 0x4768 (5 players x 0x60 at (0x450)+0x2100), timer A main loop 0x10E2 calls 0x128E / 0x116A.
- CORRECTION (10-11): the "driver drops most note-ons" bullet was wrong: the flat count ignored REF (0x81 off16 count8 = replay count events from 0xB01E+off). With REF expansion the title song is 503 s; first note per channel (s into song): ch1 0, ch5 10.6, ch9 23.8, ch3 36.0, ch7 36.8, ch12 36.9, ch10 60.8, ch4 85.8, ch8 86.0, ch11 86.0, ch0 88.6, ch6 89.6, ch2 144.2, ch15 206.9. Emulator key-ons in song 0-18 s (~100, run32/keys.txt, song starts emu 19.79 s) match the expected ~108. Sequencer + driver OK.
- MUS file layout (TITLE.MUS, 0x62CDC b): header 0x24 (sizes: tone 0x532D4, seq 0x9706, DSP 0x540), driver 0x24.., tone bank file 0x5DC0 -> RAM 0x2C000, seq file 0x59094 -> 0xB000, DSP prog file 0x6279C -> 0x171CA. Load verified. Sample 0x5692C (voice 1, 16-bit BE) is a clipped square-wave sweep then noise (a zap synth), authentic data.
- Remaining audio defects: SCSP DSP effects (EFSDL sends, DSP RAM 0x18000) not emulated, so no reverb/echo; host stream underruns (run34 windowed: 21 per 2400 VBlanks) insert 50 ms silence gaps.
- Host audio fix (10-11): host_pace() now paces on the SDL audio queue (keep ~kTarget 3528 frames = 80 ms; kMax 22050) instead of the PC clock when audio is open. Windowed run35 (2400 VBlanks): "audio stream: 0 times empty, 0 chunks dropped" (run34 before: 21 empty). TEMP debug code removed. Clip for the user: build/title-clip-emu.wav (run32/out.wav 30-60 s).
- CORRECTION (10-11): the SCSP DSP IS emulated (runtime/scsp.cpp dsp_step, after MAME) and the game uses it: from ~19.7 s (sample 868136) the driver loads "Ny t-eff.EXB" (TITLE.MUS DSP block: name 0x00-0x1F, word 0x0200 at 0x20, COEF 64 words at 0x40, MADRS 32 words at 0xC0 = 0000 2FE4 26C3 33C0 676E 4D97 6780 6F80 68E1 70E1 8000, MPRO 128x4 words at 0x100; 110 steps). RBP 12 (DSP RAM 0x18000), RBL 32768 words. EFSDL 7 on slots 2,3,5,7,8,10,11.
- BUG FIXED (10-11): the driver writes 0x780-0x7FF as 64 words (32 longs), but MADRS is only 0x780-0x7BF; scsp.cpp masked the index with & 31, so the zeros for 0x7C0-0x7FF wiped every MADRS -> all delay taps at address 0 (DSP output 0 from 20-45 s, odd output after). Now 0x7C0-0x7FF is ignored on write and reads 0.
- DSP program reads MIXS 1, 4, 6, 9, 11 only (IRA 0x21/0x24/0x26/0x29/0x2B) and writes EFREG 2, 3, 5, 7, 8. The title loop voices at 20-45 s send ISEL 15 (unread), so no wet there is authentic; from ~46 s other voices feed it (wet RMS ~1600 vs dry ~10000; run36 headless 3700 VBlanks, TEMP diag since removed). Clip build/title-clip-reverb.wav = run36/out.wav 30-62 s, sent to the user 10-11.
- Remaining-defects line above is superseded: DSP is emulated and now correct as far as tested; host underruns fixed (run35).
- Native pass 12 (10-12, other tracks): menu confirm = C (A does nothing). Script to the Practice stage select (run37, headless): --input "1700:START,1710:,2300:C,2310:,2600:DOWN,2610:,2650:DOWN,2660:,2700:C,2710:" -> "Select stage" with 3D track preview by VBlank ~2950 (main menu Start Race / Options; Start Race submenu Championship / Time Attack / Practice). RIGHT at 3100 and 3300 keeps stage 1 (backup.bin from run4 = no progress; other stages locked?). Headless --shot V1,V2 writes shot-V.png.

Headless races (script maker scratchpad race.sh: N-1 DOWNs from VBlank 3100 every 100, then C, B held): stage 5 (run40s5) and 6 (run40s6) no fatal. Stage 4 (run40s4) FATAL at 69.038 s (~VBlank 4142, ~640 VBlanks after C, load / race start) on cpu 1 (slave): "1-byte read of 0041AC99: nothing there" = r5 0029ACC9 + r0 0017FFD0; pc 060F30A0 / pr 060F30C2 = inside a routine run by task_RunNextRingEntry 060f3094 (ring 060fdf08, 0x34-byte entries, routine at +0x30); the exact function is unknown (pc only updates at polls). ## DB4 coverage cleanup (decompile chat, 10-12)
- Method (same as AMOK DB4): UnownedScan.java lists unowned byte ranges with their refs; a range referenced only by READ/DATA that looks like pool longwords is a literal pool (left alone). Code starts are picked by prologue (starts.py x86, starts_sh.py SH-2) and created in a CreateAt loop until no new start appears; misplaced bodies are fixed with SetBodyRange / AddCode / GrowToRange / ClearRange; names come from DOS twins by neighbour order and from the decompile.
- Counts (functions / code bytes, FUN_ 0 in all): LORT 741/137834 -> 782/143200, SCORWIN 1256/221067 -> 1365/245404, scordos 2292/369435 -> 2320/371798. hand files: LORT 698, SCORWIN 825, scordos 2161 lines.
- SCORWIN: 109 new functions (mostly DOS twins with the same names, plus Watcom rt_* stubs 0x433xxx-0x438xxx); dbg_DrawHeapBar 00401e7f (push 0x2d8 __CHK prologue the picker missed). win_MainWndProc 00430195 now owns its whole switch (value table 004305a4, 24 targets at 004305d2, shared epilogue 00430667): 23 -> 840 B; win_OnSysCommand 004301cf back to 73 B. menu_KeyBindPageTail 00423ac8 is dead code in SCORWIN (in DOS the same block 0x32678 is inside menu_KeyBindingPage). Left unowned on purpose: 0041fcfb (shared tail of menu_ClearLayerOrPop / menu_ClearLayer1OrPop), 0043867a (data, same bytes as DOS 607d0), 00439423 (unused import thunks).
- scordos: 31 created, 3 false entries deleted: the MSS wrappers AIL_active_sequence_count / AIL_set_sequence_tempo / AIL_register_EOF_callback really start at 0x438b0 / 0x447b0 / 0x453b0 (push-register prologue); the old entries 0x438e8 / 0x447c7 / 0x45400 were mid-function. 8 old duplicate names renamed (eng_FrameMain 211d4, wl3_PrepareRecord 155fe, mem_ResetMarksAll 4065f, vec_Cross_b 103b5, wl3_BindRoutinesByName 1ce53, text_FindAnyOfFwd 3c7b0, menu_SwitchPage2 344d7, rt_ReturnEdxZeroEax 40921).
- LORT: 41 created. GCC runtime block 0x06004000-0x06004650: boot_JumpToEntry 06004000 (jumps to 06005e8a), variable shifts rt_AshrVar 060041ea / rt_ShiftJumpTable 0600428c (shl) / rt_ShlVar2 06004330 / rt_LshrVar 060043d4 (each: 1..32 check, then a jump into a 32-op chain through a short offset table), rt_UDiv32 06004478 (div1/rotcl), rt_MovStrLoop 06004562 and rt_StackCheckA/B 06004600 / 06004628 (no refs, dead). mesh_CullFacesMarkVerts_asm 0600d850 / mesh_CullFacesMarkVertsRange_asm 0600d8b6 (register-arg helpers of rend_MeshBoundShorts, unsure), moincam_ComputeAnchors 060e9fe4 (= PC 0x39499). cam_ComputeFrameParams duplicate: 060f6bc0 renamed cam_ComputeFrameParamsFov. Left: pools, the offset table 0600434a, 060083a0 (unreachable after a bra), 0600ea94 (bt into a pool, original oddity).
- Gotchas: AddCode stops at jmp / noreturn, so pass every switch case target as its own start; check which function owns a jump table before adding cases (the WndProc cases first went to win_OnSysCommand). ClearRange.java uses Long.decode: pass 0x-prefixed addresses. SCORWIN PE VirtualSize is 0: file offset = addr - 0x401000 + 0x400. Script output paths need forward slashes.
